At the same time, evaluation remains fragmented. Faithfulness, localization, robustness, computational cost, and human trust are often measured with different protocols. The review therefore emphasizes not only what each method contributes, but also which gap it leaves open and which later methods attempt to close that gap.

</details>


### [99] [DreamFly: Causal Memory and Receding-Horizon Diffusion Planning for Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12308)
*Yan Deng,Fei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamFly是一个基于扩散模型的航拍视觉语言导航(VLN)框架，通过引入因果对齐的历史记忆、递推视界扩散规划和显式停止预测器LiteStop，解决了航拍导航中历史上下文有限、规划视野短和隐式终止不可靠的问题。在OpenFly基准上，DreamFly在测试集和未见集上分别取得32.04%/29.46%的SR和28.22%/23.54%的SPL，优于所有对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航拍视觉语言导航(VLN)要求智能体在部分可观测环境下整合视觉证据、规划未来动作并判断何时到达目标。虽然现有的VLA模型提供了感知到动作的范式，但将其适配到航拍导航仍面临三大挑战：历史上下文有限导致时间推理不足、规划视野短缺乏前瞻性、隐式终止机制不可靠。这些挑战促使作者提出新的航拍VLN框架。

Method: DreamFly包含三个核心组件：(1) 因果对齐的历史记忆模块，仅利用当前决策步骤之前的观测来增强当前视觉表征，实现时间推理且避免未来信息泄露；(2) 递推视界扩散规划策略，预测K步动作块但仅执行第一步后重新规划（plan-K, execute-one），利用未来动作作为辅助规划目标同时保持闭环视觉反馈；(3) LiteStop停止估计器，直接从初始全掩蔽状态的动作logits估计停止概率，将显式终止决策与动作生成解耦。整体构建在Dream-VLA基础之上。

Result: 在OpenFly基准上，DreamFly在test-seen/test-unseen分割上分别达到32.04%/29.46%的SR和28.22%/23.54%的SPL，在两个指标上均优于所有对比方法，并获得最低的导航误差。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了联合建模历史上下文、未来动作结构和显式终止对于航拍VLN任务的有效性，证明了DreamFly方法在已知和未知环境中的一致改进。

Abstract: Aerial vision-language navigation (VLN) requires an embodied agent to integrate visual evidence over time, plan future actions, and determine when it has reached a navigation goal under partial observability. Although recent VLA models offer a promising perception-to-action paradigm, adapting them to aerial navigation remains challenging due to limited historical context, short planning horizons, and unreliable implicit termination. To address these challenges, we propose DreamFly, a diffusion-based aerial VLN framework built on Dream-VLA. DreamFly introduces a causally aligned historical memory that augments the current visual representation using only observations preceding the current decision step, enabling temporal reasoning without future information leakage. We further formulate navigation as receding-horizon diffusion planning, where the policy predicts a $K$-step action chunk but executes only the first action before replanning. This plan-$K$, execute-one strategy uses future actions as auxiliary planning targets while preserving closed-loop visual feedback. Finally, LiteStop estimates the stop probability directly from action logits at the initial all-mask state, decoupling explicit termination from action generation. Experiments on the OpenFly benchmark demonstrate consistent improvements in seen and unseen environments. DreamFly achieves 32.04%/29.46% SR and 28.22%/23.54% SPL on the test-seen/test-unseen splits, respectively, outperforming all compared methods on both metrics while attaining the lowest navigation error. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of jointly modeling historical context, future action structure, and explicit termination for aerial VLN.

</details>


### [100] [AVA-Encoder: Towards Agent-Native Video Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12313)
*Chuyue Li,Jinpeng Yu,Haozhe Wang,Tian Xueyun,Zhijing Zhang,Bingnan Li,Shuqi Gu,Kan Ren,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AVA-Encoder（Agentic Video Auto-Encoder），一种通过智能体自动编码学习智能体原生视频表示的新框架。它将视频转换为知识图谱表示后再重建回视频，通过文本梯度优化实现数据无关的编码策略伪训练和测试时的数据相关KG表示精炼，解决了创造性智能体无法从高质量电影中学习的核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创造性智能体缺乏从高质量人类电影中有效学习的能力，限制了其生成影院级视频。核心障碍在于缺少一种既忠实于电影内容又能直接用于智能体推理和操作的视频结构化表示。现有视频表示方法无法兼顾内容保真度与智能体可操作性。

Method: AVA-Encoder将视频编码为知识图谱表示，再重建回视频以验证保真度。KG包含层级结构和状态节点存储结构化文本，链接的资产层存储生成的图像、音频和视频；类型化边保留文本描述与资产间的关系供智能体查询和编辑。利用视频重建差异驱动文本梯度优化框架，将评估反馈表达为自然语言更新方向，用于外层循环的Data-Independent Encoding Policy Pseudo-Training（数据无关编码策略伪训练）和内层循环的Data-Dependent KG Representation Refinement（测试时数据相关KG表示精炼）。

Result: 大量实验表明AVA-Encoder比最强外部基线提升20.7个百分点。在受控的策略单独设置下，其伪训练的镜头级Agentic Video Encoder策略性能超过人工精心调优的策略，同时节省74.3%的系统提示词元。研究还发布了完整的AVA-Encoder框架、可靠的智能体视频重建基准，以及首个高质量电影KG表示数据集。

Conclusion: AVA-Encoder通过知识图谱+文本梯度的自编码范式，成功实现了既忠实于视频内容又可直接用于智能体推理和操作的视频表示学习。该方法不仅大幅超越外部基线，还展示了自动化策略伪训练优于人工调优的潜力，为创造性智能体从电影中学习开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Creative agents still lack an effective way to learn from high-quality human films, limiting their ability to produce cinematic-grade videos. A key challenge is the absence of a structured video representation that is both faithful to film content and directly usable for agentic reasoning and manipulation. To address the challenge, we propose the Agentic Video Auto-Encoder (AVA-Encoder), a framework for learning agent-native video representations via agentic auto-encoding.
  AVA-Encoder transforms a video into a knowledge graph (KG) representation and then reconstructs it back into video. Its hierarchy and state nodes store structured text, while a linked asset layer holds generated images, audio, and video. Typed edges preserve the relations between these text descriptions and assets in a form that agents can easily understand, query, and edit. The video reconstruction differences drive a textual-gradient optimization framework, which expresses evaluation feedback as natural-language update directions for Data-Independent Encoding Policy Pseudo-Training in the outer loop and optional Data-Dependent KG Representation Refinement in the test-time inner loop.
  Extensive experiments show that AVA-Encoder improves by 20.7 percentage points over the strongest external baseline. In the controlled policy-only setting, its pseudo-trained shot-level Agentic Video Encoder policy also outperforms a carefully human-tuned policy while using 74.3% fewer system-prompt tokens. We release the complete AVA-Encoder framework, a reliable agentic video reconstruction benchmark, and the first dataset of high-quality film KG representations.

</details>


### [101] [StateFlow: Building, Evolving, and Accessing 3D World States for Previsualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12314)
*Yuyang Yin,Zixiang Li,Longxuan Deng,Hongkai Li,Shifang Zhao,Junnan Liu,Weirong Huang,Mengyu Wang,Tianxiao Fu,Yikai Wang,Peng-Shuai Wang,Xiaojie Jin,Yao Zhao,Yunchao Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StateFlow提出了一种以状态为中心的生成式预可视化框架，通过持久的可编辑3D世界来组织场景结构、演变和相机，替代一次性视频生成方式，实现更强的可控性和迭代编辑能力。该框架包含状态构建、状态演变和状态访问三个阶段，实验证明能生成高质量3D世界用于视频创作和类游戏原型制作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式预可视化方法依赖简单提示词通过一次性图像或视频合成来联合控制场景、动作、相机和时空动态等多因素，存在可控性弱、不支持迭代编辑的问题。其根本缺陷在于缺少一个显式且持久的工作状态来表示世界中的多元素（几何、外观等属性）与相机配置。

Method: StateFlow通过三个核心阶段构建状态中心框架：(1) 状态构建（State construction）：通过先验引导、冲突感知的双视角初始化将生成的2D内容提升为连贯的3D世界；(2) 状态演变（State evolution）：将用户意图转化为结构化状态转换，同时保留世界记忆，避免每次编辑都重新生成整个场景；(3) 状态访问（State access）：使用渲染反馈反射来优化相机计划，生成视觉上可行的轨迹，而非仅依赖VLM语义。

Result: 实验表明，StateFlow能够为视频创作和类游戏原型制作生成高质量的3D世界，验证了状态中心方法在预可视化任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: StateFlow证明将显式的3D世界状态作为核心工作表示，可以显著提升生成式预可视化的可控性和迭代编辑支持，为电影、游戏、建筑和城市设计等领域的预可视化工作流提供了新范式。

Abstract: Previsualization is an intermediate layer between ideas and production in film, games, architecture, and urban design. It lets creators iteratively refine scenes, actions, cameras, and spatial-temporal dynamics. Yet existing generative methods rely on simple prompts to jointly control all of these factors through one-shot image or video synthesis, offering weak controllability and limited support for iterative editing. Fundamentally, a world comprises multiple elements with geometry, appearance, and other attributes, together with cameras. Different frames are produced through local modifications or recombinations of this shared state, which is otherwise largely reused. Therefore, we argue that the missing component is an explicit and persistent working state. To address this, we present StateFlow, a state-centric framework for generative previsualization. Rather than generating videos in one shot, StateFlow uses an editable 3D world to organize scene structure, evolution, and cameras, while off-the-shelf video models enhance visual quality when higher fidelity is desired. This world is maintained as a persistent structured 3D state of scene elements and camera configurations, serving as the core working representation for previsualization. Built on this insight, StateFlow has three stages to construct, evolve, and access the world state. State construction lifts generated 2D content into a coherent 3D world through prior-guided, conflict-aware dual-view initialization, while State evolution translates user intent into structured state transitions while preserving world memory, avoiding full-scene regeneration for each edit. State access uses render-feedback reflection to refine camera plans into visually feasible trajectories, avoiding reliance on VLM semantics alone. Experiments show that StateFlow produces high-quality 3D worlds for video creation and game-like prototyping.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Adaptation of Generalist Robot Policies with Minimal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11363)
*Shreyas Kowshik,Sreyas Venkataraman,Leo Wang,Niharika Pant,Max Simchowitz,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MiDAS，一种简单的"离线到在线"强化学习策略，使预训练机器人策略仅需一条人类演示即可适应新任务，随后通过自主在线交互进一步提升性能。实验在LIBERO、RoboCasa和双机械臂YAM平台上验证，是首个从单条演示实现可靠机器人策略适应的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习的目标是从依赖特定任务的人工数据收集转向自主交互学习。然而完全自主学习在当前策略下仍困难重重：稀疏奖励和弱零样本探索使机器人几乎不可能从零发现成功行为。因此需要研究"最小数据适应"机制，即预训练策略仅需极少人类指导即可启动自主学习的可行路径。

Method: MiDAS采用简单的离线到在线强化学习方案：（1）首先在单条/少量演示上通过行为克隆将预训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型锚定到目标任务；（2）然后通过基于残差策略参数化的值函数在线强化学习进一步提升策略性能。这种两阶段设计将人类演示视为"引导"而非"监督"，后续自主交互负责真正的改进。

Result: 在LIBERO和RoboCasa基准上，MiDAS从单条演示即可恢复强任务性能，显著优于基线方法并能泛化到超出演示条件的情境。在双机械臂YAM平台上，从单条演示获得的低成功率策略出发，MiDAS在约6小时在线交互中提升策略鲁棒性并学习到新的成功行为。

Conclusion: 这是首次证明从单条任务演示就能实现可靠机器人策略适应。结果表明，最小人类引导（一条演示）足以启动自主强化学习过程，为机器人摆脱大规模任务特定数据依赖、走向自主改进提供了可行范式。

Abstract: A central goal in robot learning is to move beyond task-specific human data collection toward robots that improve through autonomous interaction. Yet fully autonomous learning remains difficult with current policies: sparse rewards and weak zero-shot exploration make it unlikely that a robot will discover successful behavior from scratch. We study minimal-data adaptation, a regime in which a pre-trained robot policy must learn a new task from as little as one demonstration followed by autonomous online interaction. This setting serves as the closest tractable proxy for fully autonomous improvement, allowing us to study whether minimal human guidance can bootstrap autonomous learning and what algorithmic ingredients make it feasible. We build MiDAS, a simple offline-to-online RL recipe that first anchors a pre-trained VLA to the target task with behavior cloning on single/few demonstrations, then improves it through value-based online RL on a residual policy parameterization. Across LIBERO and RoboCasa, MiDAS recovers strong task performance from as little as one demonstration, substantially outperforming baselines and generalizing beyond demonstrated conditions. We further evaluate MiDAS on a bimanual YAM platform. Starting from a fragile low-success policy obtained from a single demonstration, MiDAS improves its robustness and learns new successful behaviors over ~6 hours of online interaction. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of reliable robot policy adaptation from a single task demonstration.

</details>


### [103] [Top-down Traffic Scenario Generation via Joint Initial-Goal Diffusion and Trajectory Infilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11407)
*Da Saem Lee,Yash Vardhan Pant,Sebastian Fischmeister*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TrafficDiffuser框架，通过联合建模初始状态和目标状态对来生成高层级交通场景，使初始状态更具可解释性，并将轨迹生成简化为填充问题，在Argoverse 2数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通模拟器依赖给定的初始状态生成轨迹，限制了可扩展性和多样性，且数据驱动的初始状态生成缺乏可解释性；同时已知初始状态下的轨迹生成也需要学习目的地可变性。针对这些挑战，需要一种能生成高可解释性高层级交通场景的框架。

Method: 提出TrafficDiffuser，一个自顶向下的交通场景生成框架，通过联合建模初始状态和目标状态对来生成高层级交通场景。该框架使初始状态更具可解释性，并将轨迹生成简化为填充（infilling）问题，支持基于不同轨迹模式的约束以及与现有轨迹生成模型集成。

Result: 在Argoverse 2运动预测数据集上的大量实验表明，生成输出能很好地捕捉真实世界分布。除了生成目标状态外，TrafficDiffuser在智能体初始化任务中优于次优方法，速度分布距离降低55.3%，越野率降低2.8%。

Conclusion: TrafficDiffuser通过联合建模初始和目标状态对，成功实现了可解释的高层级交通场景生成，显著提升了初始状态生成的质量和交通模拟的可扩展性与多样性。

Abstract: Robust traffic simulators are crucial for developing and testing autonomous vehicles to reduce the costly, labor-intensive real-world data collection process and the need for physical presence on the road. However, existing simulators require agents' initial states to generate trajectories, which limits scalability and diversity due to restrictions on the given initial states. While data-driven agent initialization has been widely studied, the generated initial states are not interpretable in terms of why the agents are initialized at those specific locations. Given known initial states, trajectory generation is also a challenging problem, as the model must learn the variability of the destination and how agents should reach it over time. In this paper, we propose TrafficDiffuser, a top-down traffic scenario generation framework that generates high-level traffic scenarios, defined by initial and goal state pairs, by jointly modeling them. The high-level scenario generation makes initial states better interpretable and reduces trajectory generation into as simple as an infilling problem. We demonstrate how the generated high-level traffic scenarios can be used, including constraining based on different trajectory modes and integrating them with existing trajectory generation models. We conduct extensive experiments on the Argoverse 2 motion prediction dataset to evaluate how well the generated outputs capture real-world distributions. In addition to generating goal states, TrafficDiffuser outperforms the next-best approach for agent initialization, reducing speed distribution distance by 55.3% and the off-road rate by 2.8%.

</details>


### [104] [From Self-Normal-Positioning to Omni-Directional Tracking: Real-Time Surface Modeling Enabled Probe Tilt Control for Robotic Ultrasound Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11409)
*Xihan Ma,Haichong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个全向探头定向控制框架，集成RGB-D感知、局部表面建模和任务空间定向控制，使机器人超声系统能够追踪非垂直探头角度，适用于超声心动图等需要倾斜探头角度的临床检查。平均角度追踪误差1.06±0.66度，可恢复最大44.39±2.59度的非垂直倾斜角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人超声系统大多仅支持探头垂直于局部表面的正常定位，但超声心动图等检查需要非垂直探头角度才能获得诊断视图，因此需要感知局部表面并保持期望探头朝向的临床实用机器人系统。

Method: 提出全向探头定向控制框架，包含三部分：RGB-D感知用于实时采集表面信息，局部表面建模融合多视点点云并提供局部表面的二次估计（quadratic estimate），任务空间定向控制将期望成像方向相对于表面法向量编码，使探头能够追踪任意角度。

Result: 通过平面表面追踪、体模目标角度恢复和活体追踪专家选定视图进行验证。平均角度追踪误差为1.06±0.66度，系统可恢复最大44.39±2.59度的相对于表面法向量的非垂直倾斜角，并在体模和活体实验中成功获取所需心腔视图。

Conclusion: 该全向探头定向控制框架能够突破现有机器人超声系统仅支持垂直定位的限制，在保持实时表面感知的同时实现任意角度的探头定向追踪，为超声心动图等需要倾斜探头的临床应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Ultrasound (US) provides real-time, radiation-free imaging, but the image quality depends strongly on how the probe is oriented against the patient body. Robotic US can reduce operator workload and improve acquisition consistency; however, most existing systems focus on normal positioning, where the probe is maintained perpendicular to the local surface. This constraint is inadequate for examinations like echocardiography, where obtaining a diagnostic view requires a non-normal probe angle. Consequently, a clinically useful robotic system must sense the local surface in real-time and preserve the desired probe orientation. Here, we propose an omni-directional probe-orientation control framework that integrates RGB-D perception, local-surface modeling, and task-space orientation control. The surface model fuses multi-view point clouds and provides a quadratic estimate of the local surface. A desired imaging direction is then encoded relative to the normal, enabling the probe to track arbitrary angles. The framework was evaluated through flat-surface tracking, phantom target-angle recovery, and in-vivo tracking of an expert selected view. Results show that the mean angular tracking error was 1.06 +- 0.66 deg. The system recovered a non-normal tilt angle of up to 44.39 +- 2.59 deg relative to the surface normal, and acquired the desired heart chamber view in the phantom and in-vivo experiments.

</details>


### [105] [Locomotion Variability and User Experience in Smart Wheelchair Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11417)
*Sean Kille,Adina M. Panchea,Balint Varga,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工运动变异性在人机交互中的价值，提出一种保留用户自然运动结构的自主支持型共享控制策略，并通过用户研究证明：与传统的减少变异性的辅助策略相比，保留变异的辅助在任务性能相当的条件下能显著提升用户感知自主性和有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类运动天然具有变异性，且该变异性是依照任务相关性结构化的——在关键任务点更一致，在其他地方更灵活。然而在HRI中，基于模型的辅助策略通常假设人类行为是确定性的并抑制这种变异性，这可能改变交互体验并降低自主感。尽管运动变异性已逐渐被认为具有功能意义，但其在辅助交互中的刻意保留及其对用户体验的影响仍未被充分探索。

Method: 作者提出一种自主支持型共享控制策略，保留用户自然的运动结构。通过用户研究在三种条件下进行评估：无辅助、传统降低变异性的辅助、保留变异性的辅助。参与者在此三种条件下推智能电动轮椅，并比较任务性能与主观交互体验。

Result: 两种辅助模式在任务相关性能上相当，但保留自然运动变异的辅助带来了更良好的交互体验：参与者报告的感知自主性显著高于传统辅助，且感知有用性最高。

Conclusion: 变异性感知的辅助能够在物理人机协作中同时支持性能和用户自主性。更广泛而言，研究强调了设计辅助机器人系统时应尊重人类运动的具身体现结构，而非将变异性视为应被忽视或消除的噪声。

Abstract: Human movement is inherently variable, with variability structured according to task relevance: movements are typically more consistent at task-critical points and more flexible elsewhere. In human-robot interaction (HRI), however, model-based assistance strategies commonly assume deterministic human behavior and suppress such variability, potentially altering how interactions are experienced and lowering sense of agency. While movement variability is increasingly recognized as functionally meaningful, its deliberate preservation in assisted interaction, and its consequences for user experience, remain underexplored. In this paper, we empirically investigate how different assistance strategies shape human movement variability, task performance, and subjective interaction experience in a shared control setting. We introduce an autonomy-supportive shared control strategy that preserves users' natural movement structure. This approach is evaluated in a user study in which participants push an intelligent powered wheelchair under three conditions: no assistance, conventional variability-reducing assistance, and variability-preserving assistance. While task-relevant performance remained comparable across assisted modes, preserving natural movement variability led to more favorable interaction experiences. In particular, participants reported significantly higher perceived agency compared to conventional assistance and highest perceived usefulness. These findings suggest that variability-aware assistance can support both performance and user autonomy in physical human-robot collaboration. More broadly, the results highlight the importance of designing assistive robotic systems that respect the embodied structure of human movement rather than treating variability as noise to be neglected or eliminated.

</details>


### [106] [Herding End-to-End Autonomous Driving via Neuro-Symbolic Safety Guards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11451)
*Simón Patiño Idarraga,Erick Silva,Rehana Yasmin,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号安全护栏（neuro-symbolic safety guard），作为轻量级模块附加在已训练端到端驾驶代理的最终命令接口上，在执行前根据显式安全规则检查命令，仅在必要时替换为最近的安全替代方案。该护栏无需重新训练、不含可学习组件，每次干预均可执行且可追溯至触发规则。在Fail2Drive和Bench2Drive长尾基准上，以TransFuser v6（TFv6）为案例，将成功率提升15%，安全关键碰撞减少最多53%，同时保持原有驾驶评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代端到端驾驶代理虽平均性能高，但仍会违反人类驾驶员绝不会忽略的基本交通规则。根本原因在于其学习的是统计模式而非保证安全驾驶的物理条件，导致决策过程不透明、安全约束未被执行，存在结构性缺陷。

Method: 提出神经符号安全护栏：一个轻量级模块，附加在已训练代理的最终命令接口。命令到达车辆前，先对照显式安全规则进行检查，仅在必要时将其替换为最近的安全替代方案。该模块无需再训练、无新增学习组件，每次干预可直接执行且可追溯到触发规则。

Result: 在Fail2Drive和Bench2Drive长尾基准上，以SOTA模型TransFuser v6（TFv6）为案例进行评测：成功率提升15%，安全关键碰撞减少最多53%，同时保持原有驾驶评分（Driving Score）不变。

Conclusion: 神经符号安全护栏通过符号规则与神经网络的结合，在不牺牲驾驶性能的前提下有效提升安全性，实现可解释、可追溯的安全干预，为端到端驾驶代理的安全保障提供了一种轻量、即插即用的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern end-to-end driving agents can achieve high average performance yet still violate basic traffic rules that a human driver would never miss. The reason is structural: they learn statistical patterns rather than the physical conditions that guarantee safe driving, leaving their decision-making process opaque and safety constraints unenforced. We introduce a neuro-symbolic safety guard, a lightweight module that attaches to the final command interface of an already-trained agent. Immediately before a command reaches the vehicle, it checks the command against explicit safety rules and, only when necessary, replaces it with the nearest safe alternative. Each intervention is directly executable and traceable to the rule that triggered it, while the guard itself requires no retraining and adds no learned component. Evaluated on the long-tail benchmarks Fail2Drive and Bench2Drive using the state-of-the-art TransFuser v6 (TFv6) as a case study, the guard improves Success Rate by 15% and reduces safety-critical collisions by up to 53%, while preserving the original Driving Score.

</details>


### [107] [Koopman Representation of Nonlinear Virtual Environments in Kinesthetic Haptic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11461)
*Yanting Zhou,Jozsef Kövecses,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Koopman算子来表示与触觉系统交互的非线性虚拟环境，通过仿真和实验（包括Duffing振子VE和多用户研究）验证了其有效性，并利用Koopman表示进行闭环稳定性分析，相比传统基于无源性的方法更不保守、对设备建模不确定性更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多需要高精度力反馈的应用中，非线性虚拟环境的触觉反馈渲染至关重要。传统方法（如基于无源性的稳定性分析）在分析非线性VE与触觉系统闭环稳定性时过于保守，需要寻找一种更方便且保守性更小的替代表示方法。

Method: 采用Koopman算子对非线性虚拟环境进行表示，将其非线性动力学提升到更高维的线性空间中（即对所有提升状态做线性组合来表示非线性）。基于该Koopman表示进行触觉系统的闭环稳定性分析。

Result: 仿真和实验结果表明该方法能有效表示Duffing振荡器型非线性VE的动力学；多用户研究进一步证实了这一结论。Koopman表示的闭环稳定性分析安全性良好，且比传统无源性方法保守性更小。

Conclusion: Koopman算子为非线性VE提供了一种新颖的替代表示方式，使得闭环稳定性分析更加便捷且保守性降低，同时由于非线性用所有提升状态的线性组合表示，该方法对触觉设备建模的不确定性更具鲁棒性。

Abstract: Rendering haptic feedback with nonlinear virtual environments (VEs) is important in many applications that require highly accurate force feedback. This paper considers the use of the Koopman operator to represent a nonlinear VE interacting with a haptic system. Simulation and experimental results demonstrated that the proposed method provides an effective representation of the nonlinear dynamics of a Duffing-oscillator VE. A multi-user study further confirmed this conclusion. In addition, a closed-loop (CL) stability analysis is performed leveraging the Koopman representation of the nonlinear VE to access stability of the overall haptic system. This alternative way of representing nonlinear VEs enables a convenient CL stability analysis that is less conservative than traditional passivity-based methods. Since a linear combination of all lifted states is used to represent the nonlinearity, such representation is also more robust to uncertainties in the modeling of the haptic device than a traditional nonlinear model.

</details>


### [108] [Keep the Future, Drop the Rollout: RIFT for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11521)
*Chushan Zhang,Jinguang Tong,Xuesong Li,Yikai Wang,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RIFT（免回滚未来条件模型），通过实验证明世界动作模型（WAMs）在执行动作时不需要完整的迭代视频回滚轨迹，仅需未来K/V缓存表示即可。RIFT用学习到的预测token在一次骨干网络前向中构建完整的未来K/V缓存，在LIBERO上达到98.8%成功率（与回滚方法相当），同时将动作块延迟降低68.2%-89.1%，在RoboTwin 2.0上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）通过预测未来视频来条件化机器人动作，但迭代式视频回滚在部署时引入了显著的延迟开销。作者质疑：动作生成是否真的需要不断演化的回滚轨迹，还是仅需要其未来的表示（即未来缓存）？这是对WAMs部署实用性瓶颈的关键探究。

Method: 首先通过配对闭环干预实验（masking或reassigning未来缓存值）在4个WAM和全部40个LIBERO任务上分析：发现Joint和Cosmos-2可以重用固定最终清洁K/V缓存而几乎不损失性能，将缓存消费与缓存生产分离开。基于此洞察，提出RIFT框架：使用学习到的"预期token"在一次骨干网络前向中构建完整的未来K/V缓存，同时保留原始的未来读取接口。

Result: 在LIBERO上，RIFT达到98.8%成功率，接近基于回滚的Joint、IDM和LingBot-VA（98.4%-98.6%），但动作块延迟降低68.2%-89.1%。在RoboTwin 2.0上，RIFT在干净/随机场景中达到92.9%/92.6%成功率，为评估方法中最高。固定缓存重放实验中端末执行器平均位移误差仅1.7-1.9cm，成功率97.9%-98.2%。

Conclusion: 实验证明未来的K/V缓存值及其位置对执行具有敏感性，但特定模型可重用固定缓存。这一发现证实部署时无需迭代视频生成即可实现免回滚的未来条件化，RIFT通过单次前向构建完整未来缓存，在不牺牲成功率的前提下大幅降低部署延迟，为WAMs的实时机器人应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) condition robot actions on predicted futures, but iterative video rollout increases deployment latency. We ask whether action generation requires the evolving rollout trajectory or only its future representation. Across four WAMs on all 40 LIBERO tasks, paired closed-loop interventions show that masking or reassigning future-cache values changes execution and reduces success, indicating sensitivity to future values and their assigned positions. For Joint and Cosmos-2, however, replaying one fixed final-clean key/value (K/V) cache nearly preserves unmodified execution, with $1.7$ to $1.9$~cm end-effector average displacement error and $97.9\%$ to $98.2\%$ success. This separates cache consumption from production: these models can reuse a fixed cache but still require iterative rollout to construct it. We therefore propose RIFT (\emph{Rollout-free Imagination via Future Tokens}), which uses learned anticipation tokens to construct a complete future K/V cache in one backbone pass while retaining the original future-read interface. On LIBERO, RIFT achieves $98.8\%$ success, close to rollout-based Joint, IDM, and LingBot-VA at $98.4\%$ to $98.6\%$, while reducing action-chunk latency by $68.2\%$ to $89.1\%$. On RoboTwin~2.0, RIFT reaches $92.9/92.6\%$ on clean/randomized scenes, the highest observed among the evaluated methods. These results support rollout-free future conditioning without iterative video generation at deployment.

</details>


### [109] [RoadWeaver: Large-Scale Lane-Level HD Map Generation from Scratch for Autonomous Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11580)
*Yueyuan Li,Zexi Chen,Weijie Xi,Mingyang Jiang,Songan Zhang,Hanyang Zhuang,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoadWeaver 是一个从零生成多样化、大规模车道级高精地图的粗到细框架，先合成全局道路布局，再扩展为连通路网，最后构建具有拓扑一致性的车道级几何。实验表明其可达率达99.8%，死端率10.7%，端点对齐误差0.24米，相比SOTA方法端点对齐误差降低94.4%，生成完整高精地图仅需1.39-3.50秒，可直接部署于驾驶模拟器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶仿真需要多样化且可扩展的车道级高精地图来支持复杂路网上的长时程评估，但现有方法要么依赖手工或重建的真实地图（可扩展性受限），要么仅生成局部道路结构而非完整高精地图，缺乏从零生成大规模完整高精地图的能力。

Method: 提出RoadWeaver粗到细框架，分三步：首先合成全局道路布局，其次将其扩展为连通的道路网络，最后构建具有拓扑一致性车道连通性的车道级几何。

Result: RoadWeaver实现了99.8%的可达率、10.7%的死端比例和0.24米的端点对齐误差。相比SOTA生成方法，端点对齐误差降低94.4%，同时生成完整高精地图仅需1.39-3.50秒。

Conclusion: RoadWeaver生成的地图可直接部署于驾驶模拟器，为自动驾驶系统未来的闭环评估提供可扩展的仿真环境。训练代码和开箱即用的实现将在论文被接收后发布。

Abstract: Autonomous driving simulation requires diverse and scalable lane-level HD maps to support long-horizon evaluation across complex road networks. Existing approaches either rely on handcrafted or reconstructed real-world maps, which limits scalability, or generate only local road structures rather than complete HD maps. We present RoadWeaver, a coarse-to-fine framework for from-scratch generation of diverse, large-scale HD maps. RoadWeaver first synthesizes a global road layout, expands it into a connected road network, and then constructs lane-level geometry with topologically consistent lane connectivity. Experimental results show that RoadWeaver achieves a 99.8\% reachability, a 10.7\% dead-end ratio, and an endpoint alignment error of 0.24 m. Compared with SOTA generation methods, it reduces endpoint alignment error by 94.4\% while generating complete HD maps in 1.39--3.50 s. The generated maps can be directly deployed in driving simulators, providing scalable simulation environments for future closed-loop evaluation of autonomous driving systems. The training code and an out-of-the-box implementation of RoadWeaver will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [110] [Video2Track: From Real-World Interaction Videos to Steerable Adversarial Closed-Track Testing for Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11592)
*Mengjie Tian,Xinrui Zhang,Tianyu Li,Peizhi Zhang,Guirong Zhou,Haojie Feng,Junpeng Huang,Qixiang Zhang,Lu Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Video2Track框架，将真实道路驾驶视频中的交互场景迁移为可控的、对抗性的封闭赛道测试场景。该系统通过视觉语言模型提取视频中的场景语义并用RAG映射到封闭赛道拓扑库，再通过条件扩散模型生成多智能体轨迹，并利用Stackelberg博弈调节交互强度，实现可控风险和交互风格的自动驾驶系统验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的封闭赛道测试大多依赖标准化协议或预定义轨迹，导致交互过于脚本化，无法复现公共道路上交通的自然复杂性，难以满足安全关键场景验证对真实性与可控性的双重需求。

Method: Video2Track由两个耦合模块组成：(1) 场景语义映射模块——利用视觉语言模型从驾驶视频中提取结构化语义，并通过检索增强生成（RAG）将语义锚定到封闭赛道拓扑库中，识别兼容的地图片段与交互锚点；(2) 动态交互测试模块——基于锚定的拓扑与锚点，通过条件扩散模型生成多样化的多智能体轨迹，并以参数化对抗目标的Stackelberg博弈调控交互强度。

Result: 封闭赛道实验表明，该框架能够忠实复现具有代表性的真实世界交互场景，并生成可执行、风险水平和交互风格可控的场景变体，为自动驾驶系统验证提供了可扩展的方案。

Conclusion: Video2Track证明了将真实世界的视频交互场景系统性迁移到封闭赛道测试的可行性，为自动驾驶系统验证提供了兼顾真实性与可控性的可扩展方法论，弥补了现有标准化测试在自然交互复杂度复现上的不足。

Abstract: Closed-track testing plays a fundamental role in the verification and validation of automated driving systems (ADS), particularly for safety-critical scenarios, by enabling reproducible evaluation under controlled conditions. However, most existing approaches still rely on standardized protocols or predefined trajectories, leading to overly scripted interactions and limited ability to reproduce the natural complexity of public-road traffic. To address this limitation, we propose Video2Track, a framework that transfers real-world interactive driving scenarios from videos into steerable adversarial closed-track testing. The framework consists of two tightly coupled modules. The first is a scenario semantic mapping module, which extracts structured semantics from driving videos using a vision-language model and grounds them onto a closed-track topology library via retrieval-augmented generation, thereby identifying compatible map segments and interaction anchors. The second is a dynamic interactive testing module, which conditions on the grounded topology and anchors to generate diverse multi-agent trajectories through a conditional diffusion model, while regulating interaction intensity via a Stackelberg game with a parameterized adversarial objective. Closed-track experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework can faithfully reproduce representative real-world interaction scenarios and generate executable scenario variants with controllable risk levels and interaction styles, providing a scalable approach for realistic and steerable ADS validation.

</details>


### [111] [IoT-Enabled Autonomous Maritime Navigation in Smart Ports: A Curriculum-Guided Shared Policy Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11597)
*Yuqing Lin,Rangya Zhang,Kum Fai Yuen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对智能港口中IoT自主海事设备在部分可观测、高密度交通条件下的船载自主导航问题，提出了一种基于课程引导的强化学习框架，采用共享循环策略以增强时间推理、部署可扩展性和边缘层决策鲁棒性。集中训练作为离线设计策略，导航动作完全在船载执行。实验表明该方法在导航可靠性、碰撞避免和训练稳定性方面优于基线方法，并能有效泛化到未见的高密度场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能港口基础设施日益依赖IoThings（IoT）赋能的自主海事设备，在拥挤航道中确保可靠的船载导航智能已成为安全、可扩展运行的关键挑战。现有方法在处理部分可观测性和密集交通条件下面临困难，需要一种既能保证边缘级决策能力又具备扩展性的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种课程引导的强化学习框架：1）采用共享循环策略（shared recurrent policy）增强时间推理能力；2）采用集中式训练作为离线设计阶段策略，而所有导航动作完全在船载边缘执行，符合IoT边缘智能范式；3）通过课程学习（curriculum learning）逐步引导智能体从简单到复杂场景训练，提升鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Result: 在多个真实港口环境的广泛仿真中，该方法相比标准基线方法显著提升了导航可靠性、碰撞避免能力和训练稳定性，并能有效泛化到先前未见的高密度场景。

Conclusion: 课程引导的共享学习为智能港口中IoT自主海事设备的可扩展部署提供了一种实用的解决方案，验证了集中训练-边缘执行范式在部分可观测动态环境中的有效性。

Abstract: As smart port infrastructures increasingly rely on autonomous maritime devices enabled by the Internet of Things (IoT), ensuring reliable onboard navigation intelligence has become a critical challenge for safe and scalable operations in congested waterways. This paper investigates onboard autonomous navigation for such IoT devices under partial observability and dense traffic conditions. A curriculum-guided reinforcement learning framework with a shared recurrent policy is developed to enhance temporal reasoning, deployment scalability, and robustness of edge-level decision-making. Centralized training is adopted as an offline design-time strategy, while all navigation actions are executed fully onboard, consistent with IoT edge intelligence paradigms. Extensive simulations in multiple realistic port environments demonstrate that the proposed approach improves navigation reliability, collision avoidance, and training stability compared with standard baseline methods, and generalizes effectively to previously unseen high-density scenarios. The results indicate that curriculum-guided shared learning provides a practical solution for scalable deployment of IoT-enabled autonomous maritime devices in smart port operations.

</details>


### [112] [StellaVLA: In-Context Structured Demonstration for Generalizable Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11671)
*Siyu Xu,Yunke Wang,Zijian Wang,Dihao Zhu,Chenghao Xia,Chengbin Du,Daochang Liu,Tao Huang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StellaVLA 是一个视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的测试时适应框架，通过条件化单个检索到的结构化演示（含任务计划、子目标描述和3D运动言语化），实现零注释成本的OOD适应，支持跨具身迁移，推理时零延迟。在VLA-Arena排行榜（2026年8月1日）以0.63分排名第一，LIBERO上98.8%成功率，LIBERO-Plus上85.1%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在分布外（OOD）场景下性能会显著崩溃，传统适应方法需要收集更多数据并进行微调，成本高昂。作者旨在提出一种无需额外数据采集和微调的测试时适应方法。

Method: 提出StellaVLA框架：1）自动化离线pipeline将原始轨迹转换为结构化演示（任务计划、子目标描述、言语化3D运动），零人工注释成本；2）测试时以单条检索演示作为上下文引导，让策略推理任务而非模仿像素轨迹；3）并行双训练设计（joint action-and-language objective）在训练时内化推理能力，推理时仅使用动作专家确保实时高频控制无延迟。

Result: 在VLA-Arena排行榜（2026年8月1日）以0.63总分排名第一，超越π0.5（0.44）和LingBot-VLA（0.22）；LIBERO上平均成功率98.8%，LIBERO-Plus上成功率为85.1%；真实机器人基准验证了可使用人类/机器人演示及人-机器人（XR）演示作为上下文结构化演示来适应OOD任务。

Conclusion: StellaVLA通过将"做什么"（模仿行为）升级为"为什么"（结构化的推理指令），实现高效的测试时OOD适应，兼具跨具身迁移能力和低推理延迟，为VLA模型在实际场景中的部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can follow instructions and manipulate objects, but their performance often collapses out of distribution (OOD), when the scene, viewpoint, or object differs from training. Adapting to each new situation typically requires collecting more data and fine-tuning. We present StellaVLA, a framework that instead adapts at test time by conditioning on a single retrieved demonstration. The key idea is to move beyond imitating what an expert did and instead convey why: an automated offline pipeline converts each raw trajectory into a structured demonstration, e.g., a task plan, sub-goal descriptions, and verbalized 3D motion, at zero human-annotation cost. Provided as in-context guidance, this structured demonstration lets the policy reason about the task rather than mimic a pixel trajectory, which also makes it transferable across embodiments (real-robot, human-hand, or XR demonstrations). A parallel dual-training design internalizes this reasoning during training through a joint action-and-language objective, while inference uses the action expert alone, preserving real-time, high-frequency control with no added latency. On the VLA-Arena leaderboard(Aug 1, 2026), StellaVLA ranks first with an overall score of 0.63, versus 0.44 and 0.22 for the strong prior models ($π_{0.5}$ and LingBot-VLA), and it further leads on LIBERO with 98.8% average success rate and LIBERO-Plus with 85.1% success rate. Our real-robot benchmark demonstrates that StellaVLA can use both human/robot demos and human-to-robot (XR) demos as in-context structured demonstration to help VLA model adapt to OOD tasks.

</details>


### [113] [ContactIPM: A Structure-Exploiting Interior-Point Solver for Contact-Implicit Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11731)
*Yucheng Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ContactIPM是一种新的原始-对偶方法，将接触隐式轨迹优化的阶段式最优控制因子分解与原始-对偶一致性相结合，通过屏障耦合弹性内点松弛处理互补约束，并利用Riccati递归求解约化牛顿系统。相比CRISP和IMPACT等接触专用MPCC求解器，ContactIPM在多数基准测试中速度更快（2.17-8.87倍于CRISP，2.96-4.91倍于IMPACT于多数任务）且成功率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触隐式轨迹优化因互补约束产生的病态性（degeneracy）对传统原始-对偶求解器构成挑战。现有接触专用方法虽提升了鲁棒性，但未利用阶段式最优控制因子分解和原始-对偶一致性；而结构利用型最优控制求解器又未针对互补约束进行设计。因此需要一种能同时结合这两方面能力的方法。

Method: ContactIPM的核心方法包括：(1) 识别互补不等式对并将其通过屏障耦合弹性内点松弛嵌入；(2) 在阶段式（stagewise）层面消除松弛变量和对偶变量；(3) 使用Riccati递归求解约化牛顿系统；(4) 采用固定的多阶段MPCC恢复调度，从朴素初始化提供四种延拓和重启尝试；(5) 以未松弛的物理互补残差作为终止判据。

Result: 在固定CRISP基准测试中，ContactIPM比CRISP快2.17-8.87倍（20次配对时序重复），在Push Box和Push-T鲁棒性套件中成功率更高。对比IMPACT，ContactIPM在Push T上快2.96倍，在Cart Transport上快4.91倍，但在Push Box上慢4.46倍。在50次闭环Push Box rollouts（涵盖模型失配、测量噪声、初始位姿误差和状态重置）中亦进行了评估。

Conclusion: ContactIPM证明了接触专用MPCC求解方法与结构利用型最优控制求解器的能力可以成功结合于单一原始-对偶框架中，在多数基准场景下实现显著的速度提升和更高的成功率，尽管在个别场景（如Push Box对比IMPACT）存在性能劣势，表明该方法在部分任务上仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Contact-implicit trajectory optimization avoids prescribing contact sequences, but yields mathematical programs with complementarity constraints (MPCCs) whose degeneracy challenges conventional primal--dual solvers. Existing contact-specific methods improve robustness to this degeneracy but do not leverage a stagewise optimal-control factorization and primal--dual consistency, while structure-exploiting optimal-control solvers are not designed for complementarity constraints. We show that these capabilities can be combined in a single primal--dual method. ContactIPM identifies complementary inequality pairs, embeds them through a barrier-coupled elastic interior relaxation, eliminates slack and dual variables stagewise, and solves the reduced Newton system using a Riccati recursion. A fixed multi-phase MPCC recovery schedule provides four continuation and restart attempts from naive initializations, while termination is gated by the unrelaxed physical complementarity residual.
  We compare ContactIPM with two contact-specific MPCC solvers, CRISP and IMPACT, using matched benchmark conditions and common post-solve acceptance criteria. On four fixed CRISP benchmark cases, ContactIPM is $2.17$--$8.87\times$ faster over 20 paired timing repetitions per case and achieves higher success on the Push Box and Push-T robustness suites. Against IMPACT, ContactIPM is \(2.96\times\) faster on Push T and \(4.91\times\) faster on Cart Transport, but \(4.46\times\) slower on Push Box. In 50 closed-loop Push Box rollouts spanning model mismatch, measurement noise, initial-pose errors, and state resets,

</details>


### [114] [G0.5: One Autoregressive Stream for Robot Reasoning and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11739)
*Yicheng Liu,Zibin Dong,Baijun Ye,Tianyuan Yuan,Tao Jiang,Anqi Yang,Shicheng Cao,Haonan Liu,Yue Sun,Zihan Guo,Xiao Liu,Dong Ke,Changxun Pan,Chenru Wu,Tailai Cheng,Xiaoshu Ren,Xinlei Zhang,Jianning Cui,Zijie Zhao,Haoyu Zhang,Kaiming Xu,Haodong Yang,Bowen Zhang,Jiahui Niu,Shaoting Zhu,Shiduo Zhang,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出G0.5，一个预训练的自回归视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，使用单一Transformer解码器在单一目标下同时生成推理token和动作token，替代了传统"VLM+独立动作专家"的组合范武，并在7个独立评测范式中均超越现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型普遍采用"预训练VLM+单独训练的流匹配动作专家"的架构，这使VLM仅充当上下文编码器而非真正的决策者。作者希望让VLM真正参与决策，使预训练语言能力直接迁移到物理行为控制上。

Method: G0.5包含三个关键组件：（1）可学习的跨具身动作分词器，将异构机器人动作映射为共享词汇表；（2）原生思维链流，将任务分解、目标定位和动作提示与动作token交错排列；（3）视觉记忆模块，通过视觉编码器注入多秒历史信息。推理与动作共享同一套权重，在单一自回归目标下联合训练。

Result: G0.5在7个独立评测范式中超越SOTA：真实世界微调R1lite/R1pro（76.7%，优于π0.5的53.3%和GR00T-N1.7的24.4%）；2025 BEHAVIOR挑战赛（31.4% vs π0.5的26.3%和冠军26.1%）；DROID后训练零样本迁移（82.5%）；LIBERO语言跟随抓取（98.9%）；RoboTwin 2.0（93.3%）；SimplerEnv-Bridge（87.3%）。模型在大型机器人数据集和VQA样本上联合预训练。

Conclusion: 通过让推理与动作在单模型单目标下共享权重，预训练VLM的能力可自然迁移到物理行为执行上，提示词能直接控制动作粒度、任务时域和分布外场景处理而无需额外训练，验证了"统一自回归决策"范式的可行性与扩展性。

Abstract: The prevailing recipe for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models couples a pretrained VLM with a separately trained flow-matching action expert. This makes the VLM a context encoder rather than a decision-maker. We introduce G0.5, a pretrained autoregressive VLA in which a single transformer decoder emits reasoning and action tokens under a single objective. Three components make this tractable at foundation-model scale: a learnable cross-embodiment action tokenizer that maps heterogeneous robot actions into a shared vocabulary; a native chain-of-thought stream interleaving task decomposition, object grounding, and action hints with action tokens; and a visual memory module that injects multi-second history through the vision encoder. Because reasoning and action share a single set of weights, the pretrained VLM's capabilities carry over to physical behavior: the model follows instructions closely, and prompts directly steer action granularity, task horizon, and out-of-distribution scene handling without further training. Pretrained on a large collection of robot datasets together with VQA samples, G0.5 surpasses state-of-the-art models across 7 independent regimes: real-world fine-tuning on R1lite and R1pro robots (76.7\% vs.\ 53.3\% for $π_{0.5}$ and 24.4\% for GR00T-N1.7), the 2025 BEHAVIOR Challenge on 50 long-horizon household mobile manipulation tasks using a generalist policy (31.4\% vs.\ 26.3\% for $π_{0.5}$ and 26.1\% for the challenge winner), DROID post-training followed by zero-shot transfer to an unseen environment and objects (82.5\%), a language-following Pick-and-Place benchmark, LIBERO (98.9\%), RoboTwin 2.0 (93.3\%), and SimplerEnv-Bridge (87.3\%).

</details>


### [115] [Policy-Induced Hand Priors in Humanoid Dual-Arm Manipulation: Diagnosing and Mitigating Initial-Pose Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11769)
*Chaeyeon Jung,Juyoun Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了VLA策略在仿人双机械臂操作中依赖初始位姿的问题，量化了策略诱导的"手部先验"（HandPriorScore、残余手偏置、目标响应性），揭示初始位姿与策略之间存在强交互作用，并证明扩大训练集初始位姿覆盖范围及针对性数据增强可显著提升鲁棒性和手部选择行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLA策略被期望在不同初始配置下稳健运行，但聚合任务成功率可能掩盖特定姿态下的失败和不恰当的手部选择。现有研究缺乏对VLA策略初始位姿依赖性的系统量化分析，特别是在仿人双臂操作场景中。

Method: 提出HandPriorScore、残余手偏置（residual hand bias）和目标响应性（target responsiveness）三个量化指标，用于刻画初始条件依赖的早期手部偏好（策略诱导的手部先验）。在多个策略和17种初始配置上进行系统评估，并探讨腕部相机观测对手部选择的影响。通过扩大初始位姿覆盖范围、针对低性能配置进行定向数据增强，以及比较不同训练数据构成来验证鲁棒性改进。

Result: 发现强初始位姿-策略交互作用：同一姿态在不同策略间成功率差异显著，单一策略在不同姿态间性能波动大。特定初始手臂配置可抑制或诱导不对称手部偏好，方向和强度因策略而异。腕部相机观测影响手部选择与任务性能。扩大初始位姿覆盖率显著提升鲁棒性；针对低性能配置的定向增强可提升其成功率。足够的仿真任务暴露有益，真实或辅助数据的效果取决于姿态覆盖、仿真比例和观测可用性。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了位姿条件化手部先验，识别出局部初始手臂配置作为手部选择行为的因果控制手柄，并证明数据覆盖和训练构成对初始位姿鲁棒性的影响，为VLA策略在仿人双臂操作中的鲁棒性设计提供了量化框架和实用指导。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies are expected to operate robustly across variations in the robot's initial configuration, yet aggregate task success can conceal pose-specific failures and inappropriate hand selection. This work investigates initial-pose dependence in VLA-based humanoid dual-arm manipulation. We characterize the initial-condition-dependent early hand preference as a policy-induced hand prior and quantify it using HandPriorScore, residual hand bias, and target responsiveness. Evaluations across multiple policies and 17 initial configurations reveal strong initial-pose--policy interactions: the same pose produces substantially different success rates across policies, while a single policy exhibits large performance variation across poses. Specific initial arm configurations can suppress or induce an asymmetric hand preference, with the resulting effect varying in direction and strength across policies. Wrist-camera observations also influence hand selection and task performance. Expanding initial-pose coverage in the training dataset substantially improves robustness, while targeted augmentation around a low-performing configuration increases its success rate. Comparisons across training configurations show that sufficient exposure to the target simulation task is beneficial, whereas the effect of real or auxiliary data depends on pose coverage, simulation ratio, and observation availability. These findings characterize a pose-conditioned hand prior, identify a localized initial arm configuration as a causal handle on hand-selection behavior, and demonstrate how data coverage and training composition affect initial-pose robustness.

</details>


### [116] [Enhancing Visual Domain Robustness in Behaviour Cloning via Saliency-Guided Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11870)
*Zheyu Zhuang,Ruiyu Wang,Nils Ingelhag,Ville Kyrki,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboSaGA是一种面向视觉行为克隆的显著性引导叠加增强方法，通过策略驱动的显著性在像素级别动态调整增强强度，在不破坏任务关键信息的前提下对非关键区域进行激进增强，从而提升模型对视觉域偏移（阴影、干扰物、背景、光照变化）的鲁棒性，同时保持域内性能不下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的图像增强方法（如随机裁剪、颜色抖动）在视觉行为克隆任务中面对显著的视觉域偏移（阴影、干扰物、背景变化）时效果有限；已有的叠加类增强方法在计算机视觉中表现出泛化优势，但由于行为克隆需要保留任务关键语义、时空关系和智能体-目标交互，这些方法并不直接适用。因此需要一种既能利用叠加增强的优势又不破坏任务关键信息的针对性方法。

Method: RoboSaGA属于叠加增强（superimposition）家族，核心机制是使用策略驱动的显著性图（saliency）在像素级别动态调整增强强度：对于任务无关区域施加激进的增强，而对于任务关键区域则保留原始信息。该方法无需修改现有网络结构，也不需要引入额外的学习目标，可直接无缝集成到现有架构中。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的实验中，RoboSaGA在保持域内性能的同时，显著提升了对视觉域偏移的鲁棒性，包括干扰物和背景变化、光照变化以及阴影变化等多种场景。

Conclusion: RoboSaGA为视觉行为克隆提供了一种有效且即插即用的域偏移鲁棒性增强方案，在理论与实践上填补了叠加类增强方法在行为克隆领域的空白。

Abstract: In vision-based behavior cloning (BC), conventional image augmentations such as Random Crop and Color Jitter often fall short under substantial visual domain shifts, including changes in shadows, distractors, and backgrounds. Superimposition-based augmentations, which blend in-domain and out-of-domain images, have shown promise for improving generalization in computer vision, but their suitability for BC remains uncertain because task-critical semantics, spatiotemporal relationships, and agent-target interactions must be preserved. To address this, we introduce RoboSaGA, a Saliency-Guided Augmentation method within the superimposition family tailored for vision-based BC. RoboSaGA dynamically adjusts augmentation intensity at the pixel level using policy-driven saliency, enabling aggressive augmentation in task-irrelevant regions while preserving task-critical information. It integrates seamlessly into existing architectures without requiring structural modifications or additional learning objectives. Experiments in both simulated and real-world settings show that RoboSaGA preserves in-domain performance while substantially improving robustness to visual domain shifts, including distractor and background changes, as well as lighting and shadow variations. Code is available at https://github.com/Zheyu-Zhuang/RoboSaGA.

</details>


### [117] [D3D-GEN: Robot-Aware Domain-Grounded Interactive 3D World Generation for Social Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11876)
*Anh Duc Do,Volodymyr Scherbyna,Tai Duc Nguyen,Spaarsh Thakkar,Zhengcheng Shen,Teham Buiyan,Archan Misra,Linh Kästner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: D3D-GEN 是一个结合领域智能体与检索增强生成（RAG）管道的新型世界生成系统，能够根据领域描述快速生成基于领域知识、完全交互式的3D仿真世界，支持Isaac Sim和Gazebo两大主流机器人模拟器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能（Embodied AI）社交导航的训练与验证严重依赖真实感强且可仿真的模拟环境，但现有方法难以在真实感与可仿真性之间取得平衡。传统的仿真环境生成往往依赖固定的3D模型数据库，缺乏灵活性。

Method: D3D-GEN由两部分构成：1）领域智能体（domain agent）自动收集公开可访问的领域特定数据并构建持久化领域数据库；2）基于该数据库的RAG管道，通过动态查询用户提供的语义数据库来生成合理的楼层平面图和物体布局。系统不依赖任何固定的3D模型数据库，输出完全交互式的3D世界，可直接加载到Isaac Sim和Gazebo仿真器中，并提供本地Web前端以支持快速交互式世界生成。

Result: 系统已为多个常见领域（室内住宅、医院、办公室）构建了数据库，并为每个领域生成了数十个不同且合理的仿真环境。

Conclusion: D3D-GEN通过自动化领域知识收集与RAG驱动的世界合成，实现了一种无需固定模型数据库、可在真实感与可仿真性之间取得良好平衡的3D世界生成方案，为具身智能的仿真训练提供了高效且可扩展的工具。

Abstract: Training and validation of Embodied AI for social navigation critically depends on realistic simulation environments, yet many current approaches fail to find a balance between realism and simulability. We propose D3D-GEN, a novel world generation system that combines a domain agent with a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline grounded in that domain. Our system enables users to rapidly generate domain-grounded, fully interactive 3D worlds by automating both the collection of domain knowledge and the synthesis of realistic floorplans and object placements, without dependence on any fixed 3D model database. Given a domain description prompt, the research agent collects publicly accessible domain-specific data and constructs a persistent domain database. Using this database, our RAG pipeline generates plausible floorplans and object placements by dynamically querying a user-provided semantic database, which can be easily extended or modified. The output is a fully interactive 3D world loadable by the popular simulators Isaac Sim and Gazebo. With our approach, we have built databases for several common domains (indoor residential, hospital, office) and generated dozens of distinct, plausible simulation environments for each domain. We present D3D-GEN with a local web frontend that facilitates rapid, interactive world generation for robot simulation.

</details>


### [118] [Scalable Multi-Agent Maze Traversal with Local Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11895)
*Julian Rau,Jahir Argote-Gerald,Grace McFassel,Genki Miyauchi,Paul Trodden,Roderich Groß*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分布式算法，使多个智能体在未知且可能带环的图中协同导航，通过领导者-跟随者关系和领导者切换实现仅一个智能体探索，算法完备且其完工时间与最优全知策略渐近等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类似洞穴网络、管道系统等迷宫环境对未知环境下的多智能体导航构成重大挑战，特别是在通信受限的情况下。现有的多智能体导航方法在此类复杂环境中难以高效运行，需要一种既能保证完备性又具备近优性能的分布式解决方案。

Method: 提出一种分布式算法：智能体从指定的起始节点依次进入未知图，通过局部通信协调，采用领导者-跟随者关系和领导者切换机制。任意时刻仅由一个智能体执行探索，该智能体运行单智能体迷宫求解器。

Result: 证明了算法完备性，其完工时间（在智能体数量维度上）与最优全知策略渐近等价，并推导了时间和空间复杂度。仿真实验中多达625个智能体显示，随着智能体数量增加平均总能耗递减，且该方法优于所有智能体独立运行单智能体求解器的朴素基线。

Conclusion: 该分布式算法能够高效解决未知迷宫环境下的多智能体导航问题，通过单探索者机制避免冲突并保证完备性，同时在大规模智能体场景下展现出良好的可扩展性和优越的能效表现。

Abstract: Cave networks, pipe systems, and similar maze-like environments pose significant challenges for multi-agent navigation in unknown settings with limited communication. We propose a distributed algorithm that enables agents to collectively traverse an unknown, possibly cyclic graph. Agents enter sequentially at a designated start node and are tasked to localize and reach an undisclosed goal while avoiding collisions. They coordinate via local communication using leader-follower relationships and leader switching. At any moment in time, exploration is performed by only one of the agents, which runs a single-agent maze solver. We prove that the algorithm is complete, that its makespan is asymptotically equivalent (in the number of agents) to that of an optimal full-knowledge strategy, and derive its time and space complexity. Simulations with up to $625$ agents show a decreasing average sum-of-fuels as the number of agents increases and demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms a naïve baseline in which all agents independently execute the single-agent solver.

</details>


### [119] [DaViNCi: A Dataset Towards Outdoor Vision-and-Language Navigation with Continuous Actions and Dynamic Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11901)
*Zihao Xie,Pingrui Lai,Yitong Wu,Hua Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DaViNCi，这是首个同时引入连续与动态因素的室外视觉语言导航（VLN）数据集。该数据集包含6张地图、6,933条轨迹，要求智能体在连续动作空间中移动，同时应对不可预测的动态元素。实验表明，相比先前数据集，DaViNCi在离散环境中的成功率下降超10%，在连续环境中下降更为显著，验证了其挑战性与实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室外VLN数据集仍依赖固定的离散拓扑图构建，与快速变化的真实室外环境不符，阻碍了VLN智能体的仿真到真实场景迁移。

Method: 构建DaViNCi室外VLN数据集，同时引入连续环境和动态因素两条维度：智能体使用连续动作在室外环境移动，且需应对不可预测的动态元素。数据集涵盖6张不同地图、共6,933条轨迹，并通过对比实验分析动作粒度与动态元素的影响。

Result: 在DaViNCi上，智能体在离散环境中的成功率相比先前数据集下降超过10%，在连续环境中下降幅度更大，充分展示了该数据集的挑战性；实验同时厘清了动作粒度与动态元素对性能的影响。

Conclusion: DaViNCi证明了向更真实室外环境推进VLN研究的实用价值，为室外VLN在连续动态场景下的研究提供了重要基准。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) has progressively expanded from indoor to outdoor environments. However, existing outdoor VLN datasets still rely on fixed discrete topological graphs for construction. It fails to align with the rapidly changing real-world outdoor environments and impedes the sim-to-real transfer of VLN agents. To address this limitation, we propose DaViNCi (\textbf{D}yn\textbf{a}mic \textbf{Vi}sion-and-Language \textbf{N}avigation in \textbf{C}ont\textbf{i}nuous Environment), the first outdoor VLN dataset that simultaneously introduces both continuous and dynamic factors. The agent not only moves in the outdoor environment using continuous actions but is also required to handle unpredictable dynamic elements. The dataset encompasses six distinct maps with a total of 6,933 trajectories. Through comprehensive comparative experiments, we find that the success rate on DaViNCi decreased by more than 10\% in discrete environments compared to previous datasets. And there is an even greater decline in continuous settings, demonstrating the challenge of DaViNCi. Furthermore, we clarify the impact of action granularity and dynamic elements. These results demonstrate the practical value of DaViNCi in advancing outdoor VLN toward more realistic environments. The website is https://xzh0312.github.io/DaViNCi/.

</details>


### [120] [Learning Loco-Manipulation From SMPC Demonstrations With Sparse Offline-to-Online RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12063)
*Martin Schuck,Maks Sorokin,Simone Manni,Duy Ta,Angela P. Schoellig,Marco Hutter,Simon Le Cleac'H,Jan Brüdigam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用基于采样的模型预测控制（SMPC）在仿真中作为自动化的专家来生成大规模离线数据集，从而解决探索问题，使RL智能体仅用稀疏任务奖励即可训练，并配合低层动态稳定控制器实现超越原最优控制教师的复杂行走-操作技能，在Spot四足机器人和G1人形机器人上验证了sim-to-real的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的强化学习（RL）在复杂任务上扩展时严重受限于繁琐、缓慢的密集奖励塑形过程，这成为机器人自主性发展的瓶颈。作者希望通过消除手动奖励设计来加速技能学习。

Method: 利用基于采样的模型预测控制（SMPC）在仿真中作为可快速调节的自动化专家，生成大规模离线数据集以解决探索问题；随后使用纯稀疏任务奖励训练离策略RL智能体，并将其与低层动态稳定性控制器集成，形成高层决策与低层控制相结合的分层框架。

Result: 该方法大幅减少了学习新技能所需的时间，无需手动调节奖励；集成学习到的策略能够生成更优的行为，甚至超越原始最优控制教师；并在配备机械臂的Spot四足机器人和G1人形机器人上成功部署了复杂的行走-操作技能，验证了sim-to-real框架的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将SMPC作为自动化数据生成专家与稀疏奖励RL相结合，可以有效绕过密集奖励塑形的瓶颈，实现快速、可扩展且超越教师策略的复杂行走-操作技能学习，该框架在不同机器人形态上均具有跨仿真到现实的迁移鲁棒性。

Abstract: Integrating locomotion and manipulation is essential for robot autonomy, but scaling standard Reinforcement Learning (RL) to complex tasks is severely bottlenecked by the slow, manual process of dense reward shaping. To bypass this limitation, we leverage Sample-based Model Predictive Control (SMPC) entirely in simulation as an automated, rapidly tunable expert to generate massive offline datasets. Because this data solves the fundamental exploration problem, we can train an off-policy RL agent using purely sparse task rewards, drastically reducing the time required to learn new skills and eliminating the need for manual tuning. Integrating this high-level agent with a low-level dynamic stability controller yields more optimal behaviors that strictly align with true task objectives, ultimately allowing the learned policies to surpass the original optimal control teacher. We validate the robustness of this sim-to-real framework by successfully deploying complex loco-manipulation skills across different morphologies, including an arm-equipped Spot quadruped and a G1 humanoid.

</details>


### [121] [HandEdit: A Unified Benchmark for Egocentric Human-to-Robot Dexterous Hand Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12122)
*Zhenjie Yang,Xingyu Jiao,Guopeng Zhong,Shuzhe Yang,Shi Che,Chao Wu,Chenyu Jiang,Dongjie Zhang,Yideng Zhang,Zheng Zhang,Muyun Jiang,Haisheng Su,Shuang Jin,Donghang Zhang,Chao Yang,Li Chen,Hongyang Li,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Xiaosong Jia,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HandEdit是首个大规模体感感知（embodiment-aware）图像编辑数据集与基准，包含200M+编辑实例，覆盖26种URDF配置（13种手部、13种手-臂组合），旨在将人形手部和手臂在自我中心视角下转换为多种灵巧机器人形态，从而利用海量人类视频数据推动灵巧机器人操作学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手机器人操作是具身智能（Embodied AI）的核心，但其发展受限于遥操作数据采集成本高昂。虽然人类自我中心视频可作为可扩展替代数据源，但人形与机器人之间在外观、关节结构和相机视角上的巨大差异，使得共同训练面临显著挑战。现有通用图像编辑模型虽能力强，但缺乏体感感知先验，无法充分弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出了HandEdit，一个统一的、大规模的体感感知图像编辑数据集与基准。数据集由5个不同的源数据集构建，包含超过200M编辑实例，覆盖26种不同的URDF配置（13个仅手部、13个手-臂组合）。同时建立统一的基准协议，包含Hand-only和Hand-Arm两个轨道，支持以URDF为条件进行评估。评估采用多维度指标套件，包括通用相似性指标、基于VLM的判断以及体感感知指标，对11个代表性图像编辑基线进行广泛评估。

Result: 对11个代表性图像编辑模型进行了广泛基准评估，涵盖手部专用和手-臂组合转录任务。HandEdit提供了多维指标套件，系统性地揭示了现有图像编辑模型在体感感知转换方面的能力与不足。

Conclusion: HandEdit作为图像编辑与机器人学交叉领域的关键资源，推动了体感感知编辑模型的发展，同时使从海量人类视频数据中扩展灵巧机器人学习成为可能，为更泛化的具身智能铺平了道路。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation with dexterous hands is a cornerstone of Embodied AI, yet its progress is stifled by the high cost of collecting embodiment-aware teleoperation data. While abundant egocentric videos of human hands offer a scalable alternative, the profound discrepancies in appearance, articulation, and camera viewpoints between human and robotic data raise significant challenges for co-training. Though existing general image-editing models demonstrate strong capabilities, they lack necessary embodiment-specific priors to fully bridge this gap. In this work, we present HandEdit, a unified large-scale embodiment-aware image-editing dataset and benchmark specifically designed to transform human hands and arms into various dexterous robotic embodiments within egocentric frames. HandEdit comprises over 200M editing instances derived from five diverse source datasets, covering 26 distinct URDFs, including 13 hand-only and 13 hand-arm configurations. Alongside the dataset, we establish a unified benchmark protocol with two tracks: Hand-only and Hand-Arm, supporting URDF-conditioned evaluation. We conduct extensive evaluations of 11 representative image-editing baselines using a multi-dimensional metric suite, including generic similarity metrics, VLM-based judgment, and embodiment-aware metrics. HandEdit serves as a critical resource at the intersection of image editing and robotics: it advances embodiment-aware editing models while enabling scalable dexterous robotic learning from abundant human video data, paving the way for more generalizable Embodied AI.

</details>


### [122] [Learning-Based Behavior Planning for Automated Driving: Real-World Integration and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12198)
*Jean-Pierre Busch,Guido Linden,Jan Bergmann,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种混合规划架构，将深度学习与传统优化方法结合，用于自动驾驶车辆的运动规划，在复杂交通场景中兼顾学习方法的适应性与经典方法的可验证性和确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的运动规划方法虽能提升自动驾驶在复杂环境中的驾驶行为，但其复杂性和缺乏透明度导致可解释性、可信度和安全性保证困难。

Method: 构建深度神经网络用于理解复杂交通场景并生成驾驶行为建议，同时设计一个基于优化的监督层来验证该建议，并强制执行明确的可行性与安全性约束，形成混合规划架构。

Result: 在真实城市数据的开环研究中评估了学习型规划器的驾驶行为，讨论了闭环稳定运行的系统集成问题，并在研究车辆karl上实现了真实环境部署。

Conclusion: 该混合架构能够结合机器学习在复杂场景中的决策优势与经典方法可验证、确定性的特点，实现安全且可解释的自动驾驶运动规划。

Abstract: Recent research in machine and deep learning has shown the potential of learningbased motion planning approaches to improve the driving behavior of automated vehicles, especially in complex environments. However, their complex nature and lack of transparency can hinder explainability and trustworthiness and complicate safety assurance. Motivated by these challenges, we propose a hybrid planning architecture that combines the advantages of machine learning with the verifiability and the determinism of classical approaches. Specifically, we developed a deep neural network to interpret complex traffic scenes and propose driving behavior, while an optimization-based supervision layer validates this proposal and enforces explicit drivability and safety constraints. We evaluate the learned planner's driving behavior in open-loop studies on real-world urban data, discuss system integration aspects for stable closed-loop operation, and report results from real-world deployment on our research vehicle karl..

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Decodable but Not Accessible: Auditing Distance-Based Reliability Estimation on Disentangled Skin-Lesion Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11267)
*Duc-Vinh Tran*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文审计了基于距离的可靠性估计假设——即表征几何反映其可信度——在域对抗表征学习（通过正交强度λ控制解缠程度）下的有效性。发现表征几何随解缠强度显著变化（条件数变化两个数量级），但几乎所有可靠性估计器（包括马氏距离、余弦、k-NN、能量分数、Virtual-Logit Matching、核密度估计）的AUROC均持平且低于随机水平（约0.40）。然而有监督探针能从相同嵌入中以0.72-0.81 AUROC恢复域成员信息，说明信息并未缺失，只是对非探针式可靠性估计器不可及。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于距离的可靠性估计广泛假设表征几何能反映预测可信度，但该假设从未在直接重塑几何的训练干预（如域对抗表征学习）下被系统检验。作者旨在探索解缠强度改变表征几何后，可靠性估计是否仍然有效，以及表征中信息的可解码性与可靠性估计器的可用性之间的鸿沟。

Method: 使用解缠剂量-响应阶梯：三个检查点家族共享相同架构和16维表征，仅正交强度不同（λ=0,1,5）。评估五种几何度量（条件数等）与表征几何变化的关系，并测试八种可靠性估计器（四种基于距离、三种非基于距离，外加能量分数）。同时使用有监督线性探针从嵌入恢复域成员以验证信息存在性。

Result: 表征几何随解缠强度显著改变（条件数变化两个数量级，Kendall tau=0.84, p=2.8e-5），但可靠性估计性能在所有λ水平上持平且低于随机（ISIC-test vs. PAD-UFES AUROC≈0.40）。八种评分器中七种一致显示失败；能量分数呈孤立上升趋势但不被视为反例。有监督探针能以0.72-0.81 AUROC恢复域成员，证明相关信息未被消除。

Conclusion: 分类性能本身可能掩盖表征中信息组织方式是否被下游可靠性估计器利用的问题。信息可以在保持可解码的同时，对非探针式可靠性估计器变得基本不可达，揭示了可解码性与可估计性之间的本质鸿沟。

Abstract: Distance-based reliability estimation assumes that a representation's geometry reflects its trustworthiness, yet this assumption is rarely tested under training interventions that reshape geometry directly. We audit this assumption under domain-adversarial representation learning using a disentanglement dose-response ladder. Three checkpoint families share the same architecture and a 16-dimensional representation, differing only in orthogonality strength (lambda = 0, 1, 5). Representation geometry changed substantially with disentanglement strength: the condition number shifted by two orders of magnitude (Kendall tau = 0.84, exact p = 2.8e-5). This change was not accompanied by improved reliability estimation: Mahalanobis-distance AUROC (ISIC-test vs. PAD-UFES) remained flat and below chance (about 0.40) at every level, with no significant association with any of five geometry metrics tested. The same failure was observed for cosine-to-centroid and pooled k-nearest-neighbor scorers, plus three non-distance-based scorers: an energy-based confidence score, Virtual-Logit Matching, and a kernel density estimator. Seven of eight scorers converged on the same result; the energy-based score showed an isolated upward trend that we report but do not treat as evidence against the overall pattern. A supervised probe with no access to the training objective recovered domain membership from the identical embeddings at 0.72-0.81 AUROC across every level, showing that the relevant information was not absent from the representation. These findings indicate that classification performance alone can overlook whether information in a learned representation is organized in a form that downstream reliability estimators can use. Information can remain decodable while becoming largely inaccessible to non-probing reliability estimators.

</details>


### [124] [Hardware-Aware Deployment of Joint SAR Compression and Despeckling on FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11271)
*Cédric Léonard,Francescopaolo Sica,Martin Schulz*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文首次将联合SAR去斑与数据压缩（DDC）框架部署到基于Xilinx ZCU102 FPGA的嵌入式平台上，针对固定点算术和有限算子集进行了模型适配。系统评估了四种模型拓扑、多种精度级别以及CPU/GPU/FPGA三种平台，发现ReLU优于GDN、残差块收益甚微、FPGA能耗效率最高，为星载SAR压缩提供了实际的边缘部署流程和基于证据的设计指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代合成孔径雷达（SAR）任务的数据生成速度远超下行链路传输能力，亟需在轨数据压缩以实现近实时对地观测。虽然学习图像压缩（LIC）在率失真性能上优于现有手工编码器，且联合去斑与压缩的方法能提升表征能力，但这些方法尚未接受航天系统严格的功耗、计算和操作约束的考验。

Method: 将联合SAR去斑与数据压缩（DDC）框架部署到ZCU102 FPGA嵌入式平台，针对加速器的固定点算术和受限算子集进行模型适配；对四种模型拓扑在不同精度级别下，在CPU、GPU和FPGA三种平台上进行系统评估与对比。

Result: （1）在SAR图像上用ReLU替代传统GDN激活函数可获得更好的质量，表明针对自然图像压缩建立的设计原则不一定适用于SAR图像；（2）残差块仅带来极微小的表征收益，却消耗约10倍的计算量；（3）FPGA是所测平台中能耗效率最高的。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 研究建立了一套可用的边缘部署工作流程，并为星载SAR压缩提供了基于证据的起点，对嵌入式SAR压缩系统的模型设计与硬件选择具有直接的工程指导意义。

Abstract: Next-generation Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) missions will generate data far faster than they can downlink, making onboard data reduction essential for near-real-time Earth observation. Learned Image Compression (LIC) offers better rate-distortion performance than handcrafted codecs used operationally today, and recent work shows that simultaneously despeckling and compressing SAR imagery enables better representation capacity while unlocking higher compression rates. These methods, however, have yet to be confronted with the strict power, compute, and operational constraints of spaceborne systems. In this work, we bridge this gap by deploying a joint SAR Despeckling and Data Compression (DDC) framework on an embedded ZCU102 FPGA-based platform, introducing model adaptations that respect the accelerator's fixed-point arithmetic and limited set of supported operations. We evaluate four model topologies across precision levels and across CPU, GPU, and FPGA platforms, revealing several findings with direct design implications. We find that replacing conventional GDN activation functions with plain ReLU improves quality on SAR, suggesting that design principles established for compression of natural images do not necessarily transfer to SAR imagery. In addition, we demonstrate that residual blocks offer little representational benefit for ten times the compute, and show that the FPGA is the most energy-efficient of the platforms tested. Together, these results set a functioning edge deployment workflow and an evidence-based starting point for onboard SAR compression. The code is available at https://github.com/CedricLeon/SAR_DDC_FPGA.

</details>


### [125] [Geometry-Based Compression of Plenoptic Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11273)
*Davi R. Freitas,Gustavo L. Sandri,Ricardo L. de Queiroz*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于压缩全光点云(PPC)属性的高效方法，通过在颜色属性上应用Karhunen-Loève变换并结合多个具有帧内预测能力的属性编码器，可集成到MPEG G-PCC标准中。实验表明该方法在不同空间分辨率的PPC上优于现有RAHT和基于视频的PCC方案，作者认为其达到了新的最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全光点云(PPC)通过为每个点关联多个颜色来表示来自不同视角的光线，相比普通点云提供更高真实感，但数据量显著增大，需要高效压缩方法。现有压缩方案在PPC上性能不够理想。

Method: 该方法包含两步：首先对颜色属性进行Karhunen-Loève变换(KLT)以去除颜色通道间的相关性，然后将变换后的系数交由多个具有帧内预测能力的属性编码器进行压缩。该方案可嵌入MPEG G-PCC标准框架，兼容G-PCC现有的属性编码解决方案。

Result: 在多个不同空间分辨率的PPC数据集上进行压缩性能评估，结果显示该方法相比现有方法（如RAHT和基于视频的PCC解决方案）具有竞争力的结果，达到新的最先进性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的KLT加多编码器的PPC属性压缩方法兼容G-PCC标准，在压缩效率上超越现有方案，代表当前PPC压缩的最先进水平。

Abstract: Plenoptic point clouds (PPC) are novel data structures that represent the light from different viewing directions in order to provide a higher degree of realism to regular point clouds. This is achieved by associating each point to multiple colors instead of a single one. Here, we present a method to efficiently compress the attributes of a PPC, consisting of a Karhunen-Loève transform over the color attributes followed by multiple attribute coders with intra prediction capability. This compression scheme can be incorporated within the MPEG's geometry-based PCC (G-PCC) standard, using any of G-PCC's existing solutions for attribute coding. Compression performance assessment using PPCs of different spatial resolutions reveals competitive results in comparison to existing methods, such as RAHT-based or video-based PCC solutions. We believe our coder to be the new state of the art.

</details>


### [126] [Uncertainty-Aware and Explainable Ensemble Deep Learning Framework for Multi-Class Skin Lesion Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11280)
*Rofiqul Islam,Lilatul Ferdouse*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一个结合视觉Transformer（MaxViT-Tiny）与CNN模型（ConvNeXt-Tiny、EfficientNetV2-B0）的深度集成学习框架用于多类皮肤病变分类，通过MC Dropout估计预测不确定性，利用Grad-CAM++提供可解释性，在HAM10000数据集上达到96%准确率和99% ROC-AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤癌诊断面临类内差异大、类间相似度高、类别不平衡以及深度学习模型可解释性有限等挑战，需要构建兼具准确性、可信度和可解释性的计算机辅助诊断框架。

Method: 采用深度集成学习策略，将MaxViT-Tiny（视觉Transformer）与ConvNeXt-Tiny和EfficientNetV2-B0（CNN）三种模型进行集成；使用MC Dropout进行多次前向采样以估计预测不确定性，并通过熵阈值（<1.0）和置信度阈值（>=0.7）进行不确定性感知过滤；利用Grad-CAM++ XAI技术生成可视化热力图，突出影响模型决策的病变区域。

Result: 在HAM10000数据集上，不确定性感知过滤条件下取得96%准确率和99% ROC-AUC；宏平均精确率、召回率和F1分数分别为94%、95%和95%，三种指标的加权平均分数均为96%。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了准确、可解释且具有不确定性感知能力的皮肤病变分类，为可信赖的计算机辅助皮肤癌诊断提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Skin cancer diagnosis from dermoscopic images remains challenging due to high intra-class variability, inter-class similarity, class imbalance, and the limited interpretability of deep learning models. This paper proposes an uncertainty-aware and explainable deep learning framework for multi-class skin lesion classification. The framework combines a vision transformer model (MaxViT-Tiny) with CNN-based models (ConvNeXt-Tiny and EfficientNetV2-B0) through deep ensemble learning. Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout estimates predictive uncertainty and identifies unreliable predictions, while Grad-CAM++, an explainable AI (XAI) technique, provides visual explanations by highlighting lesion regions that influence model decisions. Evaluated on the HAM10000 dataset, the framework achieves 96% accuracy and 99% ROC-AUC under uncertainty-aware filtering (entropy < 1.0, confidence >= 0.7), with macro-average precision, recall, and F1-score of 94%, 95%, and 95%, respectively, and 96% weighted-average scores across all three metrics. The results demonstrate accurate, interpretable, and uncertainty-aware skin lesion classification for trustworthy computer-aided diagnosis.

</details>


### [127] [Physics-Informed Implicit Neural Representations for Improved Myocardial Perfusion MRI Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11282)
*Christos Tsepas,Chang Yan,Maximilian Fuetterer,Sebastian Kozerke,Cian M Scannell*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合物理信息神经网络（PINN）与时空隐式神经表示（INR）的方法，用于从动态对比增强心脏磁共振（CMR）数据中量化心肌灌注参数，在真实模拟的CMR数据集上相比先前方法展现出更高的鲁棒性和参数估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从动态对比增强CMR数据中通过示踪剂动力学多室交换模型拟合来估计心肌灌注参数是一个对噪声和采集变异性敏感的逆问题。此前提出的PINN方法虽有前景，但仍有提升空间。本文旨在通过引入时空隐式神经表示来改进PINN框架的精度、平滑性和物理一致性。

Method: 在先前PINN框架的基础上，引入时空隐式神经表示（INRs），将MR信号表示为连续的时空函数，从而扩展PINN模型。该模型直接在真实模拟的CMR数据集上进行验证，并与先前已建立的拟合方法进行比较。

Result: 在真实模拟的CMR数据集上，所提出的结合INRs的PINN方法比先前已建立的方法展现出更优的鲁棒性和参数估计精度。

Conclusion: 结合时空隐式神经表示的PINN框架能够有效提升定量灌注CMR的拟合质量，提高参数估计的准确性和稳定性，为临床心肌灌注量化提供了更可靠的替代方案。

Abstract: Quantifying myocardial perfusion from cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) can be achieved by fitting tracer-kinetic models to the dynamic contrast-enhanced MR data. However, fitting the observed data with multi-compartment exchange models, which describe the evolution of the contrast agent in the tissue, to estimate perfusion parameters is a challenging inverse problem that is sensitive to noise and acquisition variability. Previously, physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have been proposed as an alternative to conventional non-linear least squares fitting methods with promising results for quantitative perfusion CMR. In this work, we extend the previously proposed PINN framework with spatiotemporal implicit neural representations (INRs) to represent the MR signal as a continuous spatiotemporal function and to improve the accuracy, smoothness, and physical consistency of the PINN model. In realistic simulated CMR datasets, our proposed PINN with INRs demonstrates improved robustness and parameter estimation accuracy over the previously established methods. The code is available at https://github.com/q-cardIA/pinn-inr.

</details>


### [128] [Uncertainty-Aware Compositional Localization and Placement Assessment of Catheters and Tubes in Chest X-Rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11288)
*Harshil Lodhiya*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出UCompCXR组合式框架，用于胸片中导管和管路放置的评估。该框架通过检测局部导管片段、基于图聚类关联成导管实例、融合片段级尖端预测并通过精度加权高斯估计，最终对每个导管进行分类。在RANZCR CLiP数据集上比强多任务基线多检测26%的设备，假阳性减少75%，尖端不确定性校准良好（95%覆盖率=0.948），模型仅2.27M参数，可部署在资源受限的临床硬件上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸片上导管和管路放置评估是安全关键任务，但现有深度学习方法存在不足：全局分类方法丢失了设备位置信息，而将所有设备分割为单一面罩的方法在导管重叠时无法进行逐设备评估。需要一种既能检测设备又能逐设备评估放置状态的组合式框架。

Method: UCompCXR采用组合式（compositional）框架，包含四个核心步骤：（1）检测局部导管片段；（2）通过基于图的聚类将片段关联为设备实例；（3）通过精度加权高斯估计融合每个片段的尖端预测；（4）对每个设备实例进行放置分类。使用MobileNetV3作为骨干网络。

Result: 在RANZCR CLiP数据集（30,083张图像，5折患者级交叉验证，带bootstrap置信区间）上，UCompCXR比共享MobileNetV3骨干的强多任务基线多检测26%的设备，假阳性减少75%。尖端不确定性校准良好（95%覆盖率=0.948）。在匹配的设备上，灾难性的定位失败大幅减少。总体尖端误差上升，但原因仅是模型发现了基线完全遗漏的设备（尤其是鼻胃管）。模型仅2.27M参数，单次前向传播即可完成推理。

Conclusion: UCompCXR通过组合式方法实现了优于现有基线的逐设备导管检测与放置评估，在检测率、假阳性率和不确定性校准方面均表现更优，同时保持了参数高效性和临床可部署性。

Abstract: Assessing catheter and tube placement on chest X-rays is safety-critical yet tedious and error-prone. Current deep learning methods either classify placement globally -- losing track of which device is where -- or segment all devices into a single mask, making per-device assessment impossible when catheters overlap. We introduce UCompCXR, a compositional framework that detects local catheter fragments, associates them into device instances via graph-based clustering, fuses per-fragment tip predictions through precision-weighted Gaussian estimation, and classifies placement per device. On the RANZCR CLiP dataset (30,083 images, 5-fold patient-level CV with bootstrap CIs), UCompCXR detects 26% more devices than a strong multi-task baseline sharing the same MobileNetV3 backbone, with 75% fewer false positives and well-calibrated tip uncertainty (95% coverage = 0.948). The aggregate tip error rises -- but only because the model finds devices the baseline misses entirely, especially nasogastric tubes. On matched devices, catastrophic localization failures drop substantially. At 2.27M parameters in a single forward pass, the model is deployable on resource-constrained clinical hardware.

</details>


### [129] [SinoDiff: Physics-Consistent Self-Supervised Diffusion for Unified Low-Dose to Standard-Dose PET Sinogram Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11514)
*Ghulam Nabi Ahmad Hassan Yar,Himashi Peiris,Sharna Jamadar,Alex Fornito,Zhaolin Chen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: SinoDiff 是一个自监督、物理一致的扩散框架，用于低剂量 PET 正弦图到标准剂量的恢复。它通过将 PET 采集模型（Poisson thinning）集成到前向扩散过程中，实现多剂量水平的统一建模，无需跨剂量重训练，并在 [18F]-FDG 和 [18F]-FDOPA 数据集上取得了与监督及自监督基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量 PET 虽能减少辐射暴露，但图像质量差影响诊断。现有方法存在明显局限：监督方法难以跨剂量泛化且需要配对数据；剂量无关的监督方法同样依赖配对 LD-SD 数据；自监督方法虽灵活但结果较差，常丢失解剖细节并使病理特征过度平滑。这些缺陷严重限制了实际临床应用。

Method: SinoDiff 提出将 PET 采集模型引入扩散前向过程，通过 Poisson thinning 对剂量依赖的计数统计进行物理一致的建模，替代传统扩散方法中的噪声模拟。逆向扩散过程估计预定义剂量水平间的 PET 信号增量，从而以单一统一模型覆盖多个剂量水平，无需重训练。此外，引入频域卷积以捕捉投影角度与探测器 bin 之间的长程依赖关系，适配 PET 正弦图特性。

Result: 在 [18F]-FDG 和 [18F]-FDOPA 两个数据集上的实验表明，SinoDiff 在多个剂量水平下取得了与监督基线和自监督基线相当的竞争力性能。

Conclusion: SinoDiff 通过物理一致的扩散建模与频域卷积设计，克服了现有 LD-PET 恢复方法在泛化性、配对数据需求和图像质量方面的局限，提供了一种无需重训练即可跨多剂量水平的统一恢复框架，具有良好的临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Low-dose positron emission tomography (LD-PET) reduces radiation exposure but leads to poor image quality and hinders diagnostic confidence. Existing supervised LD to standard-dose (SD) PET recovery methods often fail to generalise across dose variations, while dose-agnostic supervised methods require paired LD-SD data. Current self-supervised methods, although more flexible, typically produce inferior results, including loss of anatomical details and oversmoothing pathological features. These pose major limitations for practical applications. To overcome these limitations, we propose SinoDiff, a novel self-supervised, physics-consistent diffusion framework for the recovery of PET sinograms across multiple predefined dose levels. Unlike the noise simulation in traditional diffusion methods, SinoDiff integrates the PET acquisition model into the forward diffusion process via Poisson thinning, enabling physically consistent sampling/modelling of dose-dependent count statistics. During the reverse diffusion process, SinoDiff estimates the incremental change in PET signals from predefined dose levels. Therefore, SinoDiff is a single, unified model that requires no retraining across multiple dose levels. To consider the characteristics of PET sinogram, we incorporate a frequency-domain convolution to capture long-range dependencies across projection angles and detector bins. Experiments on [18F]-FDG and [18F]-FDOPA datasets demonstrate that SinoDiff achieves competitive performance against supervised and self-supervised baselines across multiple dose levels.

</details>


### [130] [Boundary-Continuous Cross-Camera RGB Mapping via Hue-Split Model Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11548)
*Yuma Kinoshita,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于色相分割的模型树方法，用于跨相机RGB颜色映射。通过沿色相坐标递归分割颜色空间，在每个节点拟合仿射颜色校正矩阵(CCM)，并用对数域误差目标与边界连续混合来消除硬分割带来的伪轮廓，显著降低log-RMSE并抑制色度间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全局单一仿射颜色校正矩阵无法捕捉不同相机间由于传感器光谱灵敏度和ISP流程差异导致的色相特异性颜色偏差，需要更精细的色相感知映射模型。

Method: 该方法递归地沿标量色相坐标分割源相机颜色空间，构建在每个节点存储仿射CCM的模型树；采用对数域误差目标拟合节点CCM；提出边界连续公式，通过沿根到叶路径混合所有节点CCM的对数域输出来避免硬色相分割带来的伪轮廓；路径混合权重在单纯形约束下优化，同时使用图表对拟合损失和显式连续性正则化项（基于位于每个学习色相阈值两侧的边界原型对）。

Result: 在Canon EOS-1Ds Mark II到Canon EOS 20D的映射实验中（使用Middlebury Registered Color Checker数据集），色相分割显著降低了相较于单一全局仿射CCM的log-RMSE；所提出的路径混合与边界原型正则化在两种光照和多种曝光条件下同时提高了准确性并抑制了学习色相阈值处的色度间隙。

Conclusion: 色相分割模型树是一种有效的跨相机颜色映射方法，通过路径混合和边界原型正则化能够同时提升颜色映射精度并消除色相阈值处的跳变伪影，优于传统全局仿射CCM。

Abstract: We propose a hue-split model-tree method for boundary-continuous cross-camera RGB mapping. Cross-camera RGB mapping aims to produce consistent color representations across cameras whose recorded RGB values differ due to sensor spectral sensitivities and image-signal processing pipelines. A common chart-based remedy is to estimate a single global affine color correction matrix (CCM), but such a global model cannot capture hue-specific discrepancies between cameras. To capture that behavior, we recursively partitions the source-camera color space along a scalar hue coordinate and builds an model tree that stores an affine CCM at every node. For fitting the node CCMs, we utilize a log-domain error objective. To prevent false contours that arise from hard hue splits, we further introduce a boundary-continuous formulation in which the prediction is obtained by blending the log-domain outputs of all node CCMs along the root-to-leaf path. The path-wise blending weights are optimized under a simplex constraint using both a chart-pair fitting loss and an explicit continuity regularizer defined on deterministic boundary prototype pairs placed just on either side of each learned hue threshold. We conducted an experiment on a Canon EOS-1Ds Mark II to Canon EOS 20D mapping using the Middlebury Registered Color Checker dataset. The results show that hue splitting substantially reduces log-RMSE over a single global affine CCM and that the proposed path blending with boundary prototype regularization simultaneously improves accuracy and suppresses chromaticity gaps at the learned hue thresholds across two illuminants and multiple exposure conditions.

</details>


### [131] [Alignment of Similarity-Transformed Images Based on Fourier--Mellin Transform Using Auxiliary Function Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11565)
*Shinji Yamashita,Yuma Kinoshita,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在亚像素精度下估计两幅图像间相似变换（平移、缩放、旋转）的算法。该方法将基于傅里叶幅度谱对数极坐标表示的尺度-旋转估计与基于辅助函数法的相位相关最大化相结合，采用两阶段估计流程：先估计尺度与旋转（不受平移影响），再在空间域利用校正后的图像对实现亚像素平移估计。仿真实验表明，该方法相比基于离散互相关的傅里叶-梅林配准方法，显著降低了尺度、旋转和平移的估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 傅里叶-梅林配准是图像配准的代表性方法，它通过最大化离散互相关进行估计，但在需要亚像素级精度时往往无法达到足够的对齐精度，因此需要开发更精确的相似变换估计方法。

Method: 该方法将两个关键组件进行整合：(i) 从对数极坐标表示的傅里叶幅度谱中估计尺度与旋转参数，此阶段不受平移影响；(ii) 基于辅助函数法的相位相关最大化技术用于亚像素级平移估计。整个流程为两阶段估计：第一阶段在频域估计尺度与旋转，第二阶段利用校正后的图像对在空间域进行亚像素平移估计。

Result: 在对随机相似变换图像对的仿真实验中，该方法在尺度、旋转和平移三个参数的估计误差上均优于基于离散互相关的傅里叶-梅林配准方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的集成方法能够实现更高精度的亚像素级图像配准，克服了传统傅里叶-梅林方法在亚像素精度估计上的局限性。

Abstract: This paper proposes an algorithm for estimating the similarity transformation, namely translation, scale, and rotation, between two images with subpixel accuracy. Image registration is a fundamental technique for aligning images acquired under different viewpoints and imaging conditions, and a representative approach based on maximizing discrete cross-correlation is the Fourier--Mellin registration. However, the Fourier--Mellin approach often fails to achieve sufficient alignment accuracy when subpixel-level estimation is required. The proposed method integrates (i) scale-and-rotation estimation from the Fourier magnitude spectrum in a log-polar representation and (ii) maximization of phase-only correlation based on the auxiliary function method. This integration enables a two-stage estimation procedure: it first estimates scale and rotation without being affected by translation, and then estimates translation with subpixel precision in the spatial domain using the corrected image pair. A simulation experiment on image pairs subjected to random similarity transformations demonstrates that the proposed method reduces estimation errors in scale, rotation, and translation compared with Fourier--Mellin-based registration methods using discrete cross-correlation.

</details>


### [132] [A comparison of CNN architectures for Alzheimer's disease detection in single-view MRI scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11762)
*Hiram Zuniga,Ulises Orozco-Rosas,Kenia Picos*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个基准，在统一的留出测试集协议下评估十种不同CNN架构（包括ResNet、DenseNet、MobileNet、EfficientNet和VGG系列模型）对阿尔茨海默病进行分类的性能。采用两阶段迁移学习和全参数微调流程，使用来自OASIS数据集的3,900张类平衡图像进行训练。VGG16表现最佳，验证准确率0.9637，测试准确率0.9533。关键发现是所有十种架构在区分非痴呆与极轻度痴呆阶段时均存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
