Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病是主要致死原因且目前无法治愈，早期检测对减缓病程进展和保持生活质量至关重要。诊断依赖病史、认知测试、体格检查和MRI脑部扫描，而深度学习适合该分类任务，因此需要系统性的基准来评估不同CNN架构在不同疾病阶段分类中的表现。

Method: 提出一个基准，在相同的留出测试集分割协议下评估十种不同CNN架构（ResNet、DenseNet、MobileNet、EfficientNet和VGG系列）。引入两阶段迁移学习与全参数微调流程，使用从OASIS数据集（含86,437张单视图MRI脑部扫描、四分类标签）中获取的3,900张类平衡子集进行训练，涵盖非痴呆、极轻度、轻度和中度痴呆四类分类。

Result: VGG16取得了最佳结果，验证准确率为0.9637，测试准确率为0.9533。关键发现是十种架构均在区分非痴呆到极轻度痴呆阶段的转变中存在一致性困难。

Conclusion: 该基准系统性地比较了多种主流CNN架构在阿尔茨海默病分类中的性能，确立了VGG16的优越性，并揭示了早期阶段（非痴呆至极轻度痴呆）分类是当前模型的共同瓶颈，为后续针对早期检测的模型设计提供了方向。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease is a leading cause of death with no cure. Therefore, early detection is critical to slow progression and preserve quality of life. Diagnosis relies on medical history, cognitive tests, physical exams, and MRI brain scans, making deep learning suitable for Alzheimer's classification. This work proposes a benchmark that evaluates ten different convolutional neural network (CNN) architectures (including ResNet, DenseNet, MobileNet, EfficientNet, and VGG family models) under the same held-out test split protocol. A two-stage transfer learning and full fine-tuning pipeline is introduced to perform training using a class-balanced subset (3,900 images) derived from the OASIS medical imaging dataset, comprising 86,437 single-view MRI brain scans labeled into four classifications of Alzheimer's disease: Non-Demented, Very Mild Dementia, Mild Dementia, and Moderate Dementia. The best results were achieved by VGG16, with a 0.9637 validation accuracy and a 0.9533 test accuracy score. A key finding documented in this work is the difficulty of classifying the transition from Non-Demented to Very Mild Demented stages, observed consistently across all ten architectures.

</details>


### [133] [ResPCC: A Loss-Resilient Neural Point Cloud Codec over Lossy Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11845)
*Xueqin Niu,Mufan Liu,Yifan Wang,Le Yang,Jun Sun,Yiling Xu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: ResPCC是首个端到端、对数据丢失具有内在鲁棒性的神经点云编解码器。它通过损失率感知的编码器（含CALM模块和SCI机制）以及解码端的MGLR和DBR模块，在5%-30%丢包率下保持高重建保真度和优越的率失真性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的点云压缩方法在理想传输条件下表现良好，但实际网络中普遍存在的丢包问题会严重扭曲隐式特征，导致坐标漂移和几何退化。因此需要设计对数据丢失具有内在鲁棒性的点云编解码器。

Method: 提出ResPCC框架：1）编码端引入Condition-Adaptive Latent Modulation (CALM)模块，根据感知的丢包率自适应调整潜在特征分布；2）引入Spatial-Channel Interleaving (SCI)机制，将通道维度的数据丢失转化为空间上分散的元素级缺失模式；3）解码端开发Mask-Aware Graph-based Latent Restoration (MGLR)模块恢复被损坏的特征；4）采用Dictionary-based Refinement (DBR)阶段将恢复的特征与规范先验对齐。

Result: 在ShapeNet和SemanticKITTI数据集上，在5%至30%的丢包率下，ResPCC相较于基线方法持续展现更优的稳定性和率失真性能，在有损条件下保持高重建保真度。

Conclusion: ResPCC为实际网络中的3D数据传输提供了可靠的解决方案，证明损失率感知和掩码引导恢复策略能有效应对点云压缩中的丢包挑战。

Abstract: Point cloud compression (PCC) is critical for efficient storage and transmission of 3D data. While recent learning-based PCC methods achieve good rate-distortion (R-D) performance, they generally rely on ideal transmission conditions. In practice, packet loss is a common issue and can severely distort latent features, causing coordinate drift and geometric degradation. To address this challenge, we present ResPCC, the first end-to-end neural point cloud codec designed to offer intrinsic resilience against data loss. Our framework is loss-rate-aware and adapts to diverse packet loss conditions. At the encoder, we introduce a Condition-Adaptive Latent Modulation (CALM) module to adjust latent feature distributions according to the perceived loss rate, as well as a Spatial-Channel Interleaving (SCI) mechanism that transforms channel-wise data extinction into spatially scattered element-wise missing patterns. At the decoder, we develop a Mask-Aware Graph-based Latent Restoration (MGLR) module, followed by a Dictionary-based Refinement (DBR) stage to recover corrupted features and align them with canonical priors. Evaluations on ShapeNet and SemanticKITTI under 5\% to 30\% packet loss rates show that ResPCC consistently delivers superior stability and R-D performance over baselines. Our framework maintains high reconstruction fidelity under lossy conditions, providing a reliable solution for 3D data transmission over practical networks. Code is available at https://github.com/starrynight314/ResPCC.

</details>


### [134] [Domain-Aware Lightweight Spectral-Grouped Convolutions for Hyperspectral Fish Freshness Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12227)
*Kazi Nabiul Alam,Pooneh Bagheri Zadeh,Akbar Sheikh-Akbari*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出SGNet（光谱分组网络），一种轻量级架构，利用分组卷积和深度可分离空间路径分离光谱与空间特征提取，结合双重注意力机制，在16天冰箱储存鲑鱼片数据集上达到97.8%分类准确率和0.64天MAE，仅需4.75M参数，参数量较ResNet-50和ViT减少5-18倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱成像（HSI）虽能无损评估鱼类新鲜度，但传统深度学习方法未充分考虑HSI数据的独特性：光谱信息主导空间纹理、标签具有序数结构、训练样本匮乏。现有方法无法充分发挥HSI数据优势，难以实现工业级实时检测。

Method: 提出SGNet架构：1）采用分组卷积和深度可分离空间通路，将光谱与空间特征提取相分离；2）设计双注意力机制，将通道级SE（挤压-激励）与空间门控耦合，自适应突出信息特征；3）构建16天冰箱储存鲑鱼片高光谱数据集用于验证。

Result: SGNet在自建数据集上达到97.8%的分类准确率和0.64天的平均绝对误差（MAE），参数量仅4.75M。消融实验验证了各组件贡献，与ResNet-50及Vision Transformers相比，参数量减少5至18倍。

Conclusion: 领域感知（domain-aware）的架构设计能够实现精确、实时的鱼类新鲜度预测，适用于工业化部署。

Abstract: Hyperspectral imaging (HSI) offers nondestructive assessment of fish freshness by detecting biochemical alterations across spectral bands. However, conventional deep learning approaches do not fully address the particular characteristics of HSI data, such as spectral dominance over spatial textures, ordinal label structure, and a small number of training samples. We propose SGNet (Spectral-Grouped Network), a lightweight architecture that separates spectral and spatial feature extraction using grouped convolutions and a depthwise spatial pathway. A dual attention mechanism that couples channel-wise squeeze-and-excitation with spatial gating adaptively highlights informative features. SGNet achieves 97.8% classification accuracy and 0.64 days mean absolute error (MAE) with just 4.75M parameters when tested on our newly developed 16-day refrigerator-stored salmon fillet dataset. Ablation studies validate the contribution of each component, while comparisons demonstrate a five- to eighteen-fold parameter reduction relative to ResNet-50 and Vision Transformers. Our findings indicate that domain-aware design supports precise, real-time freshness prediction for industrial implementation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Dynamic Governance of Multi-LLM Agent Systems for Collaborative Conversational Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11207)
*Alexander Liss,Nicholas Desmond,Santiago Gil Gallego*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Experience Orchestrator (EO)——一个控制理论驱动的治理层，用于解决两个目标对立的LLM智能体在多轮对话中因缺乏共享目标函数而导致的系统崩溃问题。在60,000次模拟中，EO通过Contextual Bandit、PID控制器和POMDP信念跟踪器三种机制，将高意图顾问联系率从46.1%提升至78.1%，其中CB变体选择解释了97%的因子间结果方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个目标结构对立的LLM智能体在多轮交互中，由于缺乏共享目标函数，会产生"对抗性崩溃"：访客屈服、站点智能体停止变化策略、对话在没有达成任何一方目标的情况下终止。作者希望探究控制理论治理层能否替代缺失的目标函数，使这类对立的智能体交互保持功能性。

Method: 在模拟金融服务场景中（站点智能体引导访客联系顾问，访客保持心理学上真实的抵抗），EO通过三个机制治理联合轨迹：1) Contextual Bandit (CB)从真实网络分析校准的内容臂中选择；2) PID控制器通过动态schema约束强制行为一致性；3) POMDP信念跟踪器维护访客意图的概率模型。通过60,000次仿真实验进行验证。

Result: EO实现了高意图顾问联系率+32百分点的提升（78.1% vs. 朴素LLM对照的46.1%）。CB变体选择解释了因子间结果方差的97%，确认治理策略而非环境初始条件决定轨迹终点。个体层面分析揭示两种截然不同的模式：对无自然转化倾向的访客，治理层是功能性系统与非功能性系统之间的分野；对几乎已对齐的访客，朴素LLM的共情默认行为已足够。

Conclusion: 治理层可以替代缺失的目标函数，但其关键结论均受限于LLM到LLM的仿真情境。PID控制器尚未针对真实人类不可预测性进行校准，在真实流量上验证EO是关键的下一步。该研究为LLM智能体对抗性交互提供了控制理论解决方案，但泛化性仍需实证检验。

Abstract: When two LLM agents with structurally opposed objectives interact across multiple turns, the absence of a shared goal function produces not competition but collapse: the visitor capitulates, the site agent stops varying its approach, and the conversation terminates without achieving either agent's stated objective. This paper asks whether a control-theoretic governance layer can substitute for that missing goal function. The Experience Orchestrator (EO) addresses this in a simulated financial services environment where a site agent guides a visitor toward advisor contact while the visitor maintains psychologically realistic resistance. EO governs the joint trajectory through three mechanisms: a Contextual Bandit (CB) that selects content arms calibrated from real-world web analytics, a PID controller that enforces behavioral consistency via dynamic schema constraints, and a POMDP belief tracker that maintains a probabilistic model of visitor intent. Across 60,000 simulations, EO achieves a +32 percentage point lift in high-intent advisor contact rate (78.1% vs. 46.1% over a naive LLM control), with CB variant selection accounting for 97% of between-factor outcome variance -- confirming that the governance policy, not environmental initial conditions, determines where trajectories end up. Persona-level analysis reveals two distinct regimes: for visitors with no natural inclination toward conversion, the governance layer is the difference between a functional system and a non-functional one; for visitors already near alignment, a naive LLM's empathetic defaults are largely sufficient. All findings are conditional on LLM-to-LLM simulation. The PID controller has not been calibrated against real human unpredictability, and validating EO on live traffic is the critical next step.

</details>


### [136] [Distribird: Literature-Informed Prior Distribution Design for Bayesian Model Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11210)
*Patrik P. Süli,György Eigner,Roland Hollós*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Distribird是一个智能体网络应用，用于自动化基于过程的模型中参数先验分布的构建。它通过多智能体管线搜索文献、提取加权报告值，并通过AIC模型选择拟合概率分布。该系统与单提示词LLM基线在先验质量上相当，但具有可追溯性、内置有效性层（拒绝超范围请求）和完全本地化推理（不向第三方LLM传输敏感数据）等关键优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管贝叶斯校准方法已发展数十年，研究者仍几乎总是使用均匀先验，主要原因是构建信息性先验需要从科学文献中提取数据，过程缓慢且需要领域与统计双重专业知识。作者希望自动化和加速这一流程，同时保证先验的可追溯性和可靠性。

Method: Distribird部署多智能体管线：搜索文献→提取并依据领域相关性加权报告值→通过AIC模型选择拟合概率分布。无文献可用时回退到无信息替代先验，并报告每个先验的证据来源和置信水平。内置有效性层拒绝超范围请求；所有语言模型调用均在本地运行，仅生成的搜索词发送至公共文献数据库。采用三种开源权重模型（Qwen3.6 27B、Gemma 4 31B、Mistral Small 4 119B）与单提示词LLM基线对比，在10个科学领域的24个参数上进行评估。

Result: 在先验质量上，完整管线与单提示词LLM基线持平。关键差异在于：每个先验都能追溯到具体论文和数值；内置有效性层在30个模型-参数组合的11个案例中拒绝了超范围请求，而单提示词基线对这些请求返回了自信但无根据的先验；所有语言模型调用均在本地执行，保护了参数描述和未发表的建模细节。

Conclusion: 作者认为，对于科学应用而言，可追溯性、有效性保障和数据隐私等属性比点估计准确性的边际提升更为重要。Distribird解决了信息性先验构建中"耗时且需要专业知识"的核心瓶颈，使贝叶斯校准实践能超越均匀先验的默认选择。

Abstract: Bayesian calibration of process-based models requires a prior distribution for each model parameter. Despite decades of methodological work, researchers almost always fall back on uniform priors. The main reason is that building informative priors from scientific literature is slow and needs both domain and statistical expertise. We present \textbf{Distribird}, an agentic web application that automates this process. Given a parameter name, physical description, and domain context, Distribird deploys a multi-agent pipeline that searches the literature, extracts and weights reported values by domain relevance, and fits a probability distribution via AIC model selection. When no literature is available, the system falls back to sensible uninformative alternatives, and clearly reports both the evidence behind and the confidence level of every prior it produces. It is designed for the problems where the models have physically interpretable parameters, where domain knowledge exists in the published literature. We evaluate the tool on 24~parameters across 10 scientific domains comparing three open-weight models (Qwen3.6 27B, Gemma 4 31B, Mistral Small 4 119B) with a single-prompt LLM baseline. On prior quality the full pipeline \emph{matches} this baseline. Every prior is traced to the specific papers and values from which it was constructed; a built-in validity layer declines to produce priors for out-of-scope requests, whereas the single-prompt baseline returns confident but unfounded priors for them in 11 of 30~model--parameter cases; and every language-model call runs locally, so no parameter description or unpublished modelling detail is transmitted to a third-party LLM provider (only generated search terms reach the public literature databases). For scientific use, we argue these properties matter more than a marginal improvement in point-estimate accuracy.

</details>


### [137] [A Forced-Structure Reduction and Verifiable Bounds for Conway's 99-Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11211)
*Aalok Thakkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了由自主AI智能体对Conway 99图问题（srg(99,14,1,2)是否存在）的系统性攻击。贡献包括：循环图约束满足率的穷举上界为68.0%；通过λ=1和μ=2的强制结构将其归约为84个顶点上的12-正则图并用CP-SAT验证；建立并验证了指定自同构轨道存在性框架；最优验证工件达到69.43%的约束满足率，且证据表明这是多个方法均无法突破的稳健边界，与开放问题本身纠缠（因任何低于4950的证明下界即构成不存在证明）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conway 99图问题是一个经典未解的组合数学问题，询问具有特定参数的强正则图srg(99,14,1,2)是否存在。该问题长期悬而未决，本文尝试利用自主AI研究智能体对该问题进行系统化、可复现的攻击，特别是在部分学分（partial-credit）评估指标下，提供可验证的中间贡献而非仅追求完整证明。

Method: (1) 对Z/99上的循环图进行穷举搜索，证明最多满足68.0%的约束（33/49个差分类），并对另一个99阶阿贝尔群得到相同上界；(2) 利用λ=1（每个邻域是完美匹配）和μ=2（外部顶点与非匹配邻居对双射）的强制结构归约，将问题等价转换为84个顶点上的12-正则图，并用CP-SAT编码验证（通过恢复唯一的srg(9,4,1,2)验证正确性）；(3) 建立指定自同构轨道存在性框架（无不动点和单不动点作用），在srg(9,4,1,2)和Paley图srg(13,6,2,3)上进行了验证；(4) 取得69.43%的最佳已验证工件。

Result: 循环图方向给出了严格的68.0%约束满足上界穷举证明；强制结构归约成功且通过小实例验证；自同构轨道框架在两个已知图上验证通过；最佳工件达到69.43%的满足率，且14种不同方法均未超过此值，提示该边界可能是稳健的。

Conclusion: 该工作未解决Conway 99图问题，但提供了一套可复现的、分层的验证性贡献和归约工具。69.43%的边界是有意义的：由于任何严格小于100%（即<4950约束）的证明下界都直接构成不存在性证明，该稳健边界问题的解决将成为领域突破。当前结果表明问题与算法可达的边界紧密纠缠，需进一步探索以缩小差距。

Abstract: Conway's 99-graph problem asks whether a strongly regular graph with parameters $\mathrm{srg}(99,14,1,2)$ exists. We report a systematic, fully reproducible attack by an autonomous AI research agent, scored under the track's partial-credit metric. Our verifiable contributions are: (1) an exhaustive proof that no circulant graph on $\mathbb{Z}/99$ satisfies more than $3366/4950=68.0\%$ of the constraints ($33$ of $49$ difference-classes), with the same ceiling for the other abelian group of order $99$; (2) a forced-structure reduction: $λ=1$ makes each neighbourhood a perfect matching and $μ=2$ puts the outer vertices in bijection with non-matched neighbour-pairs, collapsing existence to a $12$-regular graph on $84$ vertices, encoded for CP-SAT and validated by recovering the unique $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$; (3) a validated prescribed-automorphism orbit-existence framework (fixed-point-free and single-fixed-point actions, checked on $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$ and the Paley graph $\mathrm{srg}(13,6,2,3)$), and (4) a best verified artifact at $69.43\%$, with evidence that this is a robust frontier (fourteen distinct methods, none exceeding it) entangled with the open question, since any provable bound below $4950$ is a non-existence proof.

</details>


### [138] [Detecting a Route Flip Is Easier Than Knowing Whether to Fix It: Causal Route-Mediated Damage in Quantized Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11212)
*Parvel Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究Top-k混合专家（MoE）路由不连续性导致KV量化噪声触发专家切换的问题。通过四流程归因装置测算路由介导分数（RMF），发现OLMoE-1B-7B在4-bit KV量化下约31%的损害由路由介导；可部署的路由器余量能检测翻转（AUC 0.772）但无法区分有害与有益翻转，存在经验性的收益检测障碍。研究涵盖三种架构，并进行了预注册验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-k MoE路由是不连续的，部署中的数值扰动（如KV-cache量化噪声）会将token推过决策边界，导致专家激活发生变化。但此前缺乏对这种路由介导损害的因果归因方法，也不清楚能否在推断时观测到、进而选择性修复这类损害。

Method: 构建四流程归因装置：其一测算路由介导损害分数（RMF），其二用token级归因按机制分解损害，其三、其四为预注册探针在三种架构上验证。具体含受保护的BF16门控上的模拟4-bit KV量化、真实int4 KV内核实验、受控的同一检查点flag交换实验，以及预先注册的AUC检测和有符号翻转税等指标。

Result: 在OLMoE-1B-7B 4-bit KV下RMF约0.31（发现阶段0.31置信区间[0.20,0.41]，过程复现均值0.313±0.020，预注册重执行0.231）；路由器余量检测翻转AUC达0.772，但无法区分有害与有益翻转（等于随机）；有符号翻转税和符号不可分性跨模型一致；真实int4内核结果与模拟剂量曲线兼容但功效不足（95% CI[-0.111,0.394]包含零）。预注册保留集复现了分割和近相消税，但严格的不可排除性排除近乎错过。

Conclusion: 路由介导损害在4-bit KV量化下约占三分之一的量化损害，可被检测但无法在局部推断可观测的路由器统计量中区分其好坏，构成选择性修复的实证障碍。门控的归一化约定是损害量级的调节因素而非路由可恢复性机制。研究强调预注册、报告的漏报和未见样本验证，以严格约束结论的范围与强度。

Abstract: Top-k Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routing is discontinuous, so a deployment-motivated numerical disturbance -- simulated 4-bit KV-cache quantization read by a protected BF16 gate -- pushes tokens across decision boundaries and flips which experts fire. This paper proposes no new mitigation; it supplies a causal apparatus, empirical findings, and a detection-limit result. A four-run apparatus prices the route-mediated fraction (RMF) of quantization damage, a token-level attribution decomposes it by mechanism, and pre-registered probes carry the findings across three architectures. On OLMoE-1B-7B at 4-bit KV (pilot), about a third of the damage is routing-mediated: RMF ~ 0.31 (discovery 0.31 [0.20, 0.41]; process-replicated mean 0.313 +/- 0.020; pre-registered re-execution 0.231). The deployable router margin detects that a flip occurred (AUC 0.772) but cannot tell a harmful flip from a helpful one (at chance): among the tested local, inference-observable router statistics we find no predictor of a flip's loss sign above chance -- an empirical benefit-detection barrier bounding selective repair restricted to this feature family. The signed-flip tax and sign-inseparability carry cross-model; the clean-reference remedy's payout is architecture-modulated; a controlled same-checkpoint flag-swap re-scopes the gate's normalization convention to a damage-magnitude moderator, not a route-recoverability mechanism. A real int4 KV kernel yields a fraction compatible with the fake-quant dose curve but underpowered (95% CI [-0.111, 0.394] includes zero) -- ruling out gross disagreement, not an independent replication. Hypotheses, thresholds, and evaluations were pre-registered before measurement, with misses reported; a pre-registered held-out read replicates the partition and the near-cancelling tax out of sample, while the strict impossibility exclusion narrowly misses.

</details>


### [139] [Poor Man's Agentic Modeling: Simulating Large LLM-Agent Societies on a Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11215)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用低参数代理模型替代大规模LLM智能体仿真的方法：先用数百到数千次廉价查询为每个LLM智能体拟合一个低维模型，然后可在笔记本电脑上以任意规模N运行社会仿真。该方法通过"交互阶数×记忆"分类法预测替代误差的N趋势，并在多个知名LLM仿真（如EconAgent）上验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟大量LLM智能体社会子代价高昂，但研究者关心的通常是宏观层面的问题（相变行为、典型事实、随智能体数量的标度关系），而非单个智能体的认知细节。作者希望利用统计物理的观察，以低成本替代昂贵的LLM仿真。

Method: 将每个LLM智能体替换为低参数代理模型，该模型从几百到几千次廉价查询中拟合得到。引入"交互阶数×记忆"（interaction order x memory）分类法，将智能体的感知与记忆映射到有效理论和预测的替代误差N趋势。所有智能体决策均来自真实LLM（主要为DeepSeek）的诱引克隆。

Result: 在EconAgent及另外七个知名LLM仿真的忠实重实现上验证，预测的误差趋势逐格成立。两个被推翻的预测（均针对强饱和响应及其曲率）也被无自由参数的定量理论精确匹配。

Conclusion: 方法的有效性在仿真运行之前即可由智能体的感知特性决定，并可通过"交互阶数×记忆"分类法系统预测。LLM社会仿真可以低成本地在笔记本电脑上以任意规模运行，同时保持宏观层面的准确性。

Abstract: Simulating societies of many large language model (LLM) agents is expensive, yet the questions asked of such simulations are usually macroscopic: phase behaviour, stylised facts, and scaling with the number of agents $N$, not the cognition of any single agent. We turn a statistical-physics observation into a method: replace each LLM agent by a low-parameter model fitted from a few hundred to a few thousand cheap queries, then run the society at any $N$ on a laptop. Whether this works is decided before the simulation runs, chiefly by what each agent perceives. We introduce an [interaction order x memory] taxonomy that maps perception and memory to an effective theory and a predicted $N$-trend of the surrogate error. We validate it on a faithful reimplementation of the LLM macroeconomy EconAgent and seven further named LLM simulations, with agent decisions cloned from genuine LLM elicitations (primarily DeepSeek) for a few dollars; the predicted error trends hold cell by cell, and the two refuted predictions, both on a strongly saturating response and traced to its curvature, are themselves matched quantitatively by the theory with no free parameters.

</details>


### [140] [AutoWorldModel-Bench: A State-Centric Benchmark for Automated World-Model Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11216)
*Marjan Moodi,Xuankang Zhu,Fernando De Mesentier Silva,Harold Chaput,Mohammad Reza Taesiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoWorldModel-Bench基准，用于在闭环环境中评估前沿编码智能体作为自主研究者改进世界模型的能力。在8个游戏环境和统一结构化状态表示下，64个会话中Codex-5.4和Claude Opus 4.6在63个会话中改进起始模型，其中91%的胜出编辑属于非平凡的科研式修改（新目标函数、表示、推演流程或架构变更），而非超参数调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界建模领域尚无统一方案，是理想的AI编码智能体自主研究测试场景。现有智能体基准主要聚焦于"按规格工程"任务（改进方向预先指定），缺乏对开放式研究能力的评估，因此需要一个能衡量智能体自主探索改进方向的基准。

Method: 提出AutoWorldModel-Bench闭环基准：统一结构化状态表示（从8个游戏中提取ground-truth实体状态，通过共享张量格式传递），隔离动力学建模与感知，实现分钟级迭代。在固定计算预算下，编码智能体需自主改进给定的世界模型起始代码。

Result: 在64个会话中，两个前沿模型（Codex-5.4和Claude Opus 4.6）在63个会话中成功改进起始模型；91%的胜出修改为科研式创新（新目标函数、表示、推演流程或架构变更），仅少数为超参数调整。

Conclusion: AutoWorldModel-Bench为前沿编码智能体提供了开放式研究而非按规格工程任务的评估环境，能有效区分能力差异并推动自主科研能力的评测。

Abstract: World modeling is an unsettled field: architectures, training objectives, and state representations interact in complex ways, and no single recipe dominates across environments. This makes it an ideal testbed for AI coding agents acting as autonomous researchers--a setting in which the improvement direction is not specified in advance, unlike the engineering-to-spec tasks that dominate current agent benchmarks. We introduce AutoWorldModel-Bench, a closed-loop benchmark in which frontier coding agents autonomously improve a provided world-model starter under a fixed compute budget. The benchmark spans eight game environments under a unified structured-state representation--ground-truth entity state extracted from each game and consumed through a shared tensor format--which isolates dynamics modeling from perception and enables minutes-per-run iteration. Across 64 sessions, Codex-5.4 and Claude Opus 4.6 improve their starter on 63; in 91% of sessions the winning edit is a non-trivial research-style modification--a new objective, representation, rollout procedure, or architectural change--rather than a hyperparameter tweak. Our benchmark offers a setting in which frontier coding agents can be evaluated on open-ended research rather than engineering-to-spec problems.

</details>


### [141] [MaSRead: Content-Addressed Reading of Replicated Latent Stores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11218)
*Carlos Baquero,Luís Brito,João Resende*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MaSRead，一种针对潜在空间中键值缓存片段的读取机制。它通过基于词法连接的路由和硬注意力掩码解码，使之后未知的查询能够可靠地读取已合并的复制状态，而无需依赖拼接位置的寻址性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立智能体在潜在空间中推理时，可以将计算状态以键值缓存片段的形式共享而非文本。然而，多个片段合并后会在同一位置互相干扰，导致"拼接位置≠可寻址性"——即后续查询虽然在编码时未知，却无法可靠地从合并后的缓存中读取所需片段。这构成了潜在空间共享状态可读性的核心挑战。

Method: MaSRead将读取问题定向到内容本身而非位置。其方法包括：(1) 通过不透明的键控标签集合（由片段中的词导出）进行路由；(2) 对每个选中的片段在硬注意力掩码下解码，隐藏其余片段以消除干扰；(3) 利用词法连接（lexical connectivity）通过图遍历在缓存中到达多跳查询所需的多个片段。

Result: 在链式、流水线、对称、中心枢纽和自然语言五种存储结构上，MaSRead均能单独恢复已访问的片段；在无关片段不断累积时依然有效；并可迁移到另一模型家族。路由后的物化解码工作量取决于片段长度而非存储总规模，端到端工作包含与存储规模相关的路由开销以及每个访问片段的一次读取。

Conclusion: MaSRead表明，当所需片段与查询在内容上存在连接时，复制的潜在空间存储可以被后续查询选择性地读取。其局限性明确：词法路由可能遗漏断开连接的证据，答案组合受限于冻结的读取器模型。这为潜在空间共享状态的可持续、可寻址读取开辟了道路。

Abstract: Independent agents that reason in latent space can share computed state as key-value cache fragments rather than text. Merged by a conflict-free replicated data type, these fragments form a store that converges under any delivery order or duplication. Yet a later query, unknown at encode time, cannot reliably read the merged cache: colocated fragments interfere, so colocation is not addressability. MaSRead addresses the read to content. It routes through opaque keyed tag sets derived from fragment words and decodes each selected fragment under a hard attention mask that hides the rest. Under lexical connectivity, a graph walk reaches the fragments required by a multi-hop query. Across chain, pipeline, symmetric, hub, and natural-language stores, MaSRead recovers visited fragments in isolation, remains effective as unrelated fragments accumulate, and transfers to another model family. After routing, materialized decoding depends on fragment length rather than total store size; end-to-end work still includes store-dependent routing and one read per visited fragment. The limits are explicit: lexical routing can miss disconnected evidence, and answer composition remains bounded by the frozen reader. Thus a replicated latent store becomes selectively readable for later queries when the needed fragments connect to the query through content.

</details>


### [142] [From Monolithic to Modular: Segment-level Automatic Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11219)
*Nikita Kulin,Viktor Zhuravlev,Artur Khairullin,Sergey Muravyov,Ilya Makarov,Daniil Sukhorukov,Ekaterina Averkova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SAPO（Segment-Level Automatic Prompt Optimization），将提示词按角色、上下文、任务和输出格式分解为片段，基于top-5和bottom-5示例进行针对性优化，在多个基准上超越现有APO方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动提示优化（APO）方法通常对提示词进行整体重写（monolithic rewriting），这可能导致在改善某一行为的同时损害其他行为，缺少对提示词内部不同功能片段的精细化诊断与优化。

Method: SAPO将提示词分解为角色（role）、上下文（context）、任务（tasks）、输出格式（output format）四个片段，基于top-5和bottom-5示例进行针对性改进。优化循环使用单一的LLM配合静态元提示和结构化输出来完成三件事：分段、弱点分析、候选生成。其生成过程包含两阶段：（1）片段级诊断与建议提取；（2）由强弱片段信号约束的候选合成。同时设计了训练/验证协议。

Result: 在SQuADv2、TweetEval、XSUM、CommonGen和GSM8K五个数据集上，使用GPT-3.5-Turbo和GPT-4o-mini进行评测，SAPO相比于Zero-shot以及APE、OPRO、EvoPrompt、GEPA、StraGO等强APO基线获得了最佳平均得分。

Conclusion: SAPO通过片段级（segment-level）而非整体式的提示优化策略，实现了更精细、更有效的提示改进，证明将提示词分解并按片段进行定向优化可以带来优于现有整体优化方法的性能。

Abstract: Automatic Prompt Optimization (APO) often rewrites prompts monolithically, which can improve one behavior while degrading others. We present SAPO, a segment-level APO method that decomposes prompts into role, context, tasks, and output format, then applies targeted improvements based on top-5 and bottom-5 examples. The optimization loop uses one LLM with static meta-prompts and structured outputs for segmentation, weakness analysis, and candidate generation. We describe a train/validation protocol and a two-stage generation process: (1) segment-level diagnosis and recommendation extraction, (2) candidate synthesis constrained by weak/strong segment signals. Using the evaluation setup across SQuADv2, TweetEval, XSUM, CommonGen, and GSM8K on GPT-3.5-Turbo and GPT-4o-mini, SAPO achieves the best average score against Zero-shot and strong APO baselines including APE, OPRO, EvoPrompt, GEPA, and StraGO.

</details>


### [143] [LLMs in Process Diagram Engineering: From Optimal PFDs to Validated P&IDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11220)
*Timur Zakarin,Sergei Voitov,Sergei Shumilin,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出P&ID Pilot，一个端到端的AI流水线，用于流程流程图（PFD）的自动合成及其向管道及仪表图（P&ID）的自动转换。混合方法（遗传算法GA + 大语言模型LLM）在PFD生成阶段表现最佳，而基于LLM的智能体通过受限工程软件开发工具包实现100%执行成功率的P&ID转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流程流程图（PFD）及其向管道及仪表图（P&ID）的转换目前主要依赖人工完成，耗时且成本高。利用人工智能实现自动化可带来时间节省、流程自动化以及通过探索多种拓扑选项和减少人工劳动获得经济效益。

Method: 构建两阶段端到端AI流水线：第一阶段比较四种方法进行PFD合成，其中GA与LLM的混合方法被证明最优；第二阶段使用基于LLM的智能体，通过受限的工程软件开发工具包（SDK）生成并执行已验证的修改，将PFD转换为基于源图约束的P&ID。

Result: 混合GA/LLM方法在所有方法中获得最低损失值，生成有效PFD拓扑且满足出口流量参数且无工程规则违规；LLM智能体在P&ID转换阶段实现100%执行成功率，同时符合领域特定规则和参考图结构。

Conclusion: 将GA/LLM驱动的合成与LLM变换智能体耦合的统一流水线，为端到端过程设计自动化提供了可行路径，能够产生经过验证、可部署的输出，并显著减少人工工程工作。

Abstract: Nowadays, the creation of a process flow diagram (PFD) and its subsequent transformation into a piping and instrumentation diagram (P&ID) is predominantly performed manually. Applying artificial intelligence in the task could potentially lead not only to process automation and time savings, but also to financial gains by exploring numerous diagram's topology options and reducing manual labor. This research presents P&ID Pilot - a practical end-to-end AI pipeline capable of handling flowsheet developing for both stages. The first stage focuses on PFD synthesis, whereas the second is directed toward modifying the generated PFD into P&ID. After comparing four different methods, the hybrid approach combining genetic algorithms (GA) and large language models (LLM) is shown to generate the optimal valid PFD topology, achieving the lowest loss value among all the methods, while satisfying the required outlet flow parameters without engineering-rule violations. For the second stage, the proposed LLM-based agent successfully transforms the generated PFD into a source-grounded P&ID by producing validated, executable modifications through a restricted engineering software development kit, achieving 100% execution success while maintaining compliance with domain-specific rules and reference graph structures. This unified pipeline - coupling GA/LLM-driven synthesis with an LLM-based transformation agent - offers a feasible path toward end-to-end process design automation by producing validated, deployable outputs and substantially reduces manual engineering effort.

</details>


### [144] [A Conceptual Framework for Refining Influence Knowledge from Simulation Evidence in Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11221)
*Barbara da Silva Oliveira,Julien Deantoni,Nicolas Ferry*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"影响（Influences）"新概念的 conceptual framework，用于支持仿真战役的迭代和增量式细化，以解决网络物理系统（CPS）中环境介导交互（超出直接感知与执行）难以建模而导致仿真结果理解不足的问题，并通过Simulink/Gazebo协同仿真的移动机器人案例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS行为由多方利益相关者开发的领域特定产物与运行环境之间的交互涌现。仿真和协同仿真虽已成为分析CPS行为的关键手段，但许多环境介导交互（超出直接感知和执行的交互）由于复杂性、时间限制或领域经验不足而未建模，导致仿真结果难以被正确理解和利用。

Method: 提出一种利用"Influences"概念的概念框架，通过迭代和增量式的方式细化仿真战役，逐步加深对系统行为的理解。框架通过显式建模和跟踪环境中介导的交互影响来弥补未建模交互带来的认知空白。

Result: 通过一个基于Simulink/Gazebo协同仿真的移动机器人案例研究，展示了所提框架在支持仿真战役细化和提升系统行为理解方面的可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于Influences的概念框架能有效支持CPS仿真战役的迭代细化，帮助开发者在缺乏完整环境交互建模的情况下更好地理解和利用仿真结果。

Abstract: Cyber-physical systems (CPS) are typically developed by multiple stakeholders who produce artefacts tailored to their specific domains of expertise. The behaviour of these systems emerges from the interaction between those artefacts and their operational environment. Simulation and co-simulation have become essential approaches for analysing CPS behaviour and, through simulation campaigns, developers can explore system responses under changing conditions, including interactions with the environment. However, the lack of details and understanding of some environmentmediated interactions (typically the ones beyond direct sensing and actuation), which remain unmodelled due to their complexity, a lack of time, or a lack of domain experience, hinders the proper comprehension and exploitation of simulation results. To address these limitations, we propose a conceptual framework leveraging the novel concept of Influences to support the iterative and incremental refinement of simulation campaigns and deepen the understanding of the system behaviour. We demonstrate the proposed approach through a case study involving a mobile robot implemented using Simulink/Gazebo co-simulation.

</details>


### [145] [Harnessing agent memory to build lifelong AI partners for materials scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11224)
*Siyu Liu,Bo Hu,Beilin Ye,He Cao,David J. Srolovitz,Tongqi Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以持久记忆而非特定智能体实现为核心的终身AI材料科学伙伴框架。该框架通过自进化记忆存储可检视的事实和可执行的技能，在49个真实材料工具使用问题中使GPT-5.2任务成功率接近翻倍（无需更新模型参数），在元素固体状态方程计算中将正确/部分/错误结果从22/1/4改善至25/2/0并避免92%的重复错误，在13个实际材料模拟工作流中将token负担减半并将工具调用减少超过两倍。研究表明记忆可以成为超越任何单一模型或智能体栈的持久科学资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料研究中的经验（可行的脚本、可信的协议、失败的警告、新旧问题的关联判断）对可复现性和知识转移至关重要，但这些经验通常分散在笔记本、代码仓库、任务日志和个人记忆中，且很少能在AI智能体之间移植。现有AI系统围绕特定智能体实现而非持久记忆设计，导致经验无法跨模型迁移和累积，这是阻碍AI成为长期科学伙伴的根本问题。

Method: 提出一个自进化的记忆框架，将科学经验组织为两类结构化形式：可检视的事实（observations, failure boundaries, validation checks）和可执行的技能（protocols）。记忆支持检索、修订和跨模型迁移。在三种计算场景中评估：1）49个真实材料工具使用问题（138个可执行子任务）；2）元素固体状态方程计算中的波函数初始化失败处理；3）13个实际材料模拟工作流（带隙、声子、空位、功函数分析）。

Result: 三个场景均显示显著效果：1）在49个真实材料工具使用问题中，记忆使GPT-5.2任务成功几乎翻倍且无需模型参数更新；2）将波函数初始化失败转化为预执行护栏，结果从22/1/4提升至25/2/0（正确/部分/错误），避免92%重复错误；3）在13个材料模拟工作流中，记忆使聚合token负担减半，第三轮起工具调用减少超过两倍，同时保持带隙、声子、空位和功函数分析输出的物理意义。

Conclusion: 智能体记忆可以作为持久科学资产，是一种可移植、自改进的材料研究经验记录，其价值超越任何单一模型或智能体栈。这为设计终身AI科学伙伴提供了新范式：围绕持久记忆而非特定智能体实现来构建系统，使经验能够在不同模型和代理之间积累和传承。

Abstract: Materials research advances through accumulated experience - scripts that work, protocols that are trusted, warnings attached to failed calculations or experiments, and judgement that links a new question to an old result. This experience is essential for reproducibility and knowledge transfer, yet it is usually fragmented across notebooks, repositories, job logs and individual memory, and it is rarely portable across artificial-intelligence agents. Here we argue that a lifelong AI partner for materials science can be designed around persistent memory rather than around a particular agent implementation. We introduce a self-evolving memory framework that stores scientific experience as inspectable facts and executable skills, so that observations, failure boundaries, protocols and validation checks can be retrieved, revised and migrated across models. We evaluate the idea in three computational settings that expose different layers of materials-research competence. In 49 real-world materials-tool-use questions comprising 138 executable subtasks, memory nearly doubles GPT-5.2 task success without model-parameter updates. In elemental-solid equation-of-state calculations, memory converts a wavefunction-initialization failure into a pre-execution guardrail, improving outcomes from 22/1/4 to 25/2/0 Correct/Partial/Error and avoiding 92% of repeated errors. In 13 practical material simulation workflows, remembered skills and failure facts halve the aggregate trace burden (tokens) and reduce tool calls by over a factor of two by the third round, while preserving physically meaningful outputs in band-gap, phonon, vacancy and work-function analyses. These results show that agent memory can serve as a durable scientific asset; a portable, self-improving record of materials-research experience that outlives any single model or agent stack.

</details>


### [146] [Identity from the Outside: A Conceptual Framework and Research Program for AI Personality Clones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11225)
*Luc E. Brunet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从操作化角度重新审视AI"人格克隆"下的个人身份认同问题，提出身份是多重判据的混淆，给出六项可估计的因子分解，并主张长期标准应从"轨迹保真"转向"气候保真"——即克隆应以原人自我分化的方式分化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI人格克隆技术迫使人们重新思考个人身份认同问题，但传统哲学讨论受困于意识难题。作者希望撇开意识难题，在可观察、可操作、可实验的层面定义身份，并厘清"身份"这一概念所混淆的多个判据。

Method: 提出身份的六项因子分解（基质、倾向、记忆、更新动力学、上下文、外生偶然性），用状态空间形式化表达；以"不可区分度"定义为1减去评判者的辨别优势；系数的局部敏感性可通过随机消融估计。引入与lambda演算、线性类型和双仿关系的类比，提出"委托体"作为介于商品克隆与个体之间的第三类对象，并主张以"气候保真"取代"轨迹保真"作为判别标准。

Result: 给出一个条件性猜想：在关于智能体对自身持续存在信息及其自身利害后果的假设下，可版本化性倾向损害长期不可区分度。将现有实证文献映射到三个判据（目标人保真、类人一般性、个体性），并提出实验纲领。

Conclusion: 正确的长期克隆判据不是轨迹保真而是气候保真：匹配一个人可能响应的条件分布。最好的克隆是那个以原人自会自我分化的方式从原人分化的克隆。

Abstract: AI "personality clones" force a re-examination of personal identity in operational terms. Setting aside the hard problem of consciousness, we approach identity through the indiscernibility of manifestations, as assessed by an observer over a duration. We distinguish three criteria that "identity" conflates: fidelity to a target person, generic human-likeness, and individuality. We propose a six-term factorization of observed identity (substrate, dispositions, memory, update dynamics, context, exogenous contingencies), with a state-space formulation. Indiscernibility is defined as one minus a judge's distinguishing advantage, and the factorization's coefficients become local sensitivities estimable by randomized ablation. The central claim is a conditional conjecture: given hypotheses about the agent's information on its own persistence and about consequences bearing on its own stakes, versionability tends to degrade long-horizon indiscernibility. An analogy with lambda-calculus, linear typing, and bisimulation clarifies what linearity does and does not establish. Between product-clone and individual we identify a third object, the delegate: a task-limited, bounded-lifespan partial clone ending in a bandwidth-limited testament. We map the empirical literature onto the three criteria, propose an experimental program, and argue that the correct long-horizon criterion is not trajectory fidelity but climate fidelity: matching the conditional distribution of a person's possible responses. The best clone is the one that diverges from the original as the original would have diverged from itself.

</details>


### [147] [Cutting AI Datacenter Energy with Reinforcement Learning: Measured Power Control of LLM Training from One GPU to the Fleet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11226)
*Eliseo Curcio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次刻画了强化学习（GRPO/PPO）后训练在GPU上的功耗行为，并提出一种由PPO元控制器驱动的功率自适应方法：通过调整工作负载自身的生成参数来响应实测功率。在7B规模上，控制器将功率限值违规降低89.8%，同时吞吐提升18.1%、能效提升26.2%（tokens/MWh）；在72B规模上，原始控制器因切分（sharding）导致群组大小作动器失效而出现无效结果，但改用生成并发作为作动器后，实测72B工作负载实现了比静态安全基线多35.7%的输出、违规率2.27±1.08%、比不受控运行少87.2%违规。在现实测量窗口下，功耗瞬态从半秒分辨率的23.6%降至30秒的1.6%和5分钟的0；16-GPU组合集群在30秒及以上窗口零违规，峰值需求为铭牌功率的50-56%，表明约两倍超订可行。文章还量化了经济与碳影响并给出了低成本的运营商试点方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型开发高度依赖强化学习后训练（如GRPO），但其在GPU上的功耗行为从未被系统刻画。数据中心目前用静态限值和反应式节流等对工作量无感知的机制管理GPU功率，会不加区分地拖慢硬件。作者希望填补这一空白，并开发能根据实测功耗主动适配工作负载参数的自适应方法。

Method: 作者在1至4块A100上以半小时功耗遥测数据采集7B、14B、72B三种规模的GRPO训练（38万+样本）；训练一个PPO元控制器，根据实测功率自适应调整工作量自身的生成参数。针对72B场景下群组大小（group size）作动器在模型切分下失效的问题，进行作动器权威性扫掠，改用生成并发（generation concurrency）作为作动器并重建控制器，在三个复现实测72B生成型负载上验证。还考察了不同测量时间窗口下的功耗瞬态表现及16-GPU组合集群的超订可行性。

Result: 7B全500步跟踪上：功率限值违规降低89.8%，token输出提升18.1%，能效提升26.2%。72B上原控制器因切分导致作动器失效（称为"占用率对容量"原理）；重建后：比静态安全基线多35.7%输出，违规率2.27±1.08%，比不受控运行少87.2%违规，并在约束控制器中取得最佳平均吞吐和每token能耗；自适应阈值规则在三种工况之一与之匹配。72B瞬态从0.5秒的23.6%降至30秒的1.6%、5分钟为0；16-GPU集群在30秒及以上零违规，峰值需求为铭牌功率的50-56%，显示约两倍超订可行。

Conclusion: 工作量自身的生成参数可以作为有效的GPU功率调控作动器，前提是作动器在部署形态下保有足够的调节权威（并发比群组大小更稳健）。该方案在保持或提升吞吐与能效的同时大幅减少功率违规，并表明在合理的测量与运营验证下，对该类GPU工作负载进行约两倍的功率超订是可行的，兼具经济与碳减排收益。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning post-training dominates modern language-model development, yet its power behavior on GPU hardware has not been characterized, and datacenters manage GPU power with workload-blind mechanisms, static caps and reactive throttling, that slow hardware indiscriminately. We instrument GRPO training with half-second power telemetry at 7B, 14B, and 72B scales on one to four A100s (380,000+ samples), and train a PPO meta-controller that adapts the workload's own generation parameters to measured power. Against the full 500-step 7B trace, the controller cuts power-limit violations by 89.8% while increasing token output by 18.1% and energy efficiency by 26.2% (tokens per MWh). Deployed live at 72B, the same controller family yields replicated null results, diagnosed as the group-size actuator losing authority under model sharding. An actuator-authority sweep shows the same parameters applied as generation concurrency retain 17-22% power authority, isolating an occupancy-versus-volume principle; a controller rebuilt on that actuator controls a live 72B rollout-generation workload across three replications: 35.7% more output than a static safe baseline at 2.27 +/- 1.08% budget violations, 87.2% fewer violations than uncontrolled operation, and the best mean throughput and energy per token among constrained controllers, with an adaptive threshold rule matching it in one of three operating conditions. Under realistic measurement windows the original 72B transients fall from 23.6% at half-second resolution to 1.6% at 30 s and zero at 5 min; a composed 16-GPU fleet shows zero violations at 30 s and longer, with peak demand at 50-56% of nameplate. For this fleet mix, roughly twofold oversubscription of nameplate appears feasible, subject to operator validation. We quantify the economic and carbon consequences and specify a low-cost operator pilot.

</details>


### [148] [Forecasting Side Effects of Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11227)
*Chong Yong Ong,Alson Wei Jie Sim,Peixin Zhang,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究激活引导（activation steering）的副作用预测问题，通过构建67种行为跨效应矩阵，发现副作用普遍、结构化且非对称，并证明副作用的大小主要由目标行为决定，方向可通过未引导的表示以高于基线的精度预测，为安全部署提供先验审计工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导虽能有效改变模型行为且无需重训，但经常产生意外的副作用，影响安全部署。目前缺乏在实施引导前预测这些副作用的能力，因此作者提出能否在引导前预测副作用的问题。

Method: 在三个开源语言模型上构建覆盖67种行为的跨效应矩阵，系统性地评估引导不同行为对其他行为的影响；通过分析副作用的普遍性、结构性和对称性，并利用未引导的模型表征来预测副作用的方向与大小。

Result: 副作用常见、具有结构且常不对称，无法用现有相似性启发式方法解释。副作用大小主要由目标行为决定，而其方向可由未引导的模型表征预测，精度显著高于简单基线。

Conclusion: 激活引导具有系统性且可预测的副作用，这为主动安全审计和更明智地部署引导干预提供了可能。

Abstract: Activation steering modifies a language model by adding a learned direction to its hidden activations, enabling targeted behavioral changes without retraining. While effective, steering often produces unintended side effects on other behaviors, making it difficult to deploy safely. We therefore ask: can these side effects be forecasted before steering is applied? We answer this question by constructing a cross-effect matrix over a taxonomy of 67 behaviors across three open-weight language models. We find that side effects are common, structured, and often asymmetric, revealing interactions that cannot be explained by existing similarity-based heuristics. Despite this complexity, we show that side effects are largely predictable before steering is performed. Their magnitude depends primarily on the target behavior, while their direction can be forecasted from the model's unsteered representations with substantially higher accuracy than simple baselines. Our results demonstrate that activation steering has systematic and forecastable side effects, enabling proactive safety auditing and more informed deployment of steering interventions.

</details>


### [149] [Synchronizing Beliefs with Second-Order Theory-of-Mind in Human-Autonomy Teams (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11229)
*Jack Mirenzi,Henny Admoni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新定义偏好学习问题，将人类教师从被动oracle转变为主动合作者，通过教师维护学习者模型来设计高效课程，以及学习者发布二阶理解语句来同步教师模型的更新，形成人机自主团队协作框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统偏好奖励学习将人类教师视为被动oracle，只回答学习者生成的查询，忽略了教师对目标（reward）知识的独特优势。在奖励特征维度增长时，这种被动教学策略的效率劣势愈发明显，因此需要重新设计学习范式以利用教师的主动知识。

Method: 将偏好学习重新建模为人机团队协作问题，耦合两种行为模型：（1）教师维护学习者模型以设计信息丰富的课程；（2）学习者维护教师模型的二阶模型，通过发出结构化偏好约束（理解语句）使教师的模型保持同步。仿真中对比了教师主导选择与学习者主导选择、交替教师下的模型漂移、以及二阶（ToM-2）语句与均值信念语句的效果。

Result: 仿真结果表明：（1）知情教师主导的课程选择优于学习者主导的选择；（2）交替教师会导致教师模型漂移，削弱教师主导优势；（3）理解语句可以修复模型漂移带来的性能退化，且当教师对学习者的误差集中在特定方向而非均匀分布时，二阶（ToM-2）语句优于均值信念语句。

Conclusion: 偏好学习应当被视为人机团队协作问题而非单向查询过程。利用教师对目标的知识并保持双向模型同步，可以显著提升奖励学习的效率，且二阶认知建模在非对称误差条件下提供额外优势。

Abstract: Comparative feedback, asking people which of two behaviors they prefer, has become a standard way to align robot and agent behavior with human intent when the reward itself cannot be specified directly. Preference-based reward learning typically casts the human teacher as a passive oracle answering learner-generated queries. We argue this forfeits the teacher's defining advantage: knowledge of the objective. A teacher who knows the target can construct training examples more efficiently than any learner-driven acquisition strategy, an advantage that widens as the reward's feature dimension grows. However, exploiting this advantage requires an accurate model of what the learner currently knows. We therefore recast preference learning as a human-autonomy team problem coupling two behavioral models: the teacher maintains a model of the learner to design an informative curriculum, and the learner maintains a second-order model of the teacher's model, emitting structured preference constraints (understanding statements) that keep the teacher's model of the learner synchronized. In simulation, an informed teacher outperforms learner-led selection; teacher-model drift under alternating teachers erodes this advantage; and understanding statements repair it, with second-order (ToM-2) statements outperforming mean-belief statements when the teacher's error about the learner is concentrated in a particular direction rather than spread evenly.

</details>


### [150] [The Edge-based Contiguous p-median Problem with Connections to Logistics Districting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11230)
*Zeyad Kassem,Adolfo R. Escobedo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了边基连续p-中位（ECpM）问题，将道路网络划分为给定数量的紧凑且连续的辖区。引入两种二元规划模型：第一种基于切集约束并采用分支切割（B&C）算法；第二种基于最短路连续性约束（SPC），可直接用商用求解器求解。在2700+节点、3400+边的道路网上测试，SPC模型通过标准分支定界相比切集B&C实现最高17倍加速。SPC约束被证明是边基p-中位（EpM）模型的超有效不等式。此外，SPC约束模型在边基区划（EBD）问题中优于切集模型，后者在12小时内无法找到可行解而前者可求解大多数实例至最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路网络分区问题（如配送区域划分、巡逻区域划分等）在实际中具有重要意义，需要在保证辖区连续性和紧凑性的前提下进行优化。现有的p-中位模型大多仅关注距离/成本优化而未显式建模连续性，或者使用的连续性建模方法计算效率低下。本文旨在提出一种新的ECpM问题及高效的求解方法，弥补现有模型在连续性和计算效率方面的不足。

Method: 本文提出了两种二元规划模型：(1) 切集约束模型（cut set-based），采用分支切割（B&C）算法，通过分离方案在求解过程中动态生成切集约束；(2) 最短路连续性约束（SPC）模型，使用多项式数量的最短路约束来建模连续性，可直接用商用求解器求解。两个模型均整合了网络距离度量，并在边基p-中位（EpM）和边基区划（EBD）问题的框架下进行了理论分析。

Result: 在超过2700个节点和接近3400条边的道路网络上测试，模型包含超过960万个二元变量。SPC模型通过标准分支定界相比切集B&C实现最高17倍的加速。SPC约束被证明是EpM模型的超有效不等式。在EBD问题中，切集模型在12小时内无法找到可行解，而SPC模型能求解大多数实例至最优。

Conclusion: 本文表明基于最短路连续性约束（SPC）的模型在计算效率上显著优于切集约束模型，最高可达17倍加速。SPC约束作为超有效不等式提供了有价值的理论见解。同时，SPC框架扩展到边基区划（EBD）问题时也表现出强大的求解能力，而传统的切集方法在实际规模实例上几乎不可行。这为道路网络分区问题提供了更高效实用的建模与求解框架。

Abstract: This paper introduces the edge-based contiguous p-median (ECpM) problem to partition the roads in a network into a given number of compact and contiguous territories. Two binary programming models are introduced, both of which incorporate a network distance. The first model requires an exponential number of cut set-based constraints to model contiguity; it is paired with a separation scheme that usually generates only a small number of these constraints, namely, a branch-and-cut (B&C) algorithm. The second model utilizes a polynomial number of shortest-path constraints to model contiguity and can be solved with off-the-shelf solvers. The respective solution approaches are tested on road networks with over 2,700 nodes and close to 3,400 edges, yielding models with over 9.6 million binary variables. Solving the model based on shortest path contiguity (SPC) constraints via standard branch and bound attains speedups in computational time of up to 17x relative to the cut set-based B&C implementation. In addition, the SPC constraints are demonstrated to be supervalid inequalities of the edge-based p-median (EpM) model (i.e., for which contiguity is not explicitly required), meaning that they may cut off integer-feasible solutions and some, but not all, of the optimal solutions of this simpler problem. Finally, the paper explores structural insights and connections between ECpM and the edge-based districting (EBD) problem, which enforces an additional work balance criterion. An existing model that utilizes cut set-based contiguity constraints was unable to find a feasible solution within 12 hours for any of the tested instances, while an SPC-based EBD model was able to solve most of these to optimality.

</details>


### [151] [LinearKV: One Cached State Suffices for Position-Independent Caching in Hybrid LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11231)
*Yirui Liu,Ruoling Qi,Longwen Wang,Xuaner Wu,Jian Chen,Yuxin Jin,Jiawei Shao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LinearKV，一个针对混合LLM（全注意力层+线性循环层）的训练无关的位置无关缓存（PIC）框架。其核心洞见是"解耦初始化"：线性层将匹配到的K个局部状态映射为单个初始状态，而全注意力层照常拼接KV。研究表明单缓存状态作为线性层初始化器就足够，而精确组合所有K个缓存状态的代数方法（如并发工作HYPIC）不必要，且在Mamba-2架构上反而有害，质量仅恢复46.6%对比单状态初始化的86.8%，且在TTFT上单状态初始化更快（0.46x全prefill）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PIC方法均为全注意力模型设计，依赖于token索引的KV缓存进行token块匹配、KV拼接和局部重计算。而混合LLM（如GDN、Mamba-2等）用线性递归替代大部分注意力层，只暴露固定大小的状态，没有token索引的KV可拼接或修复，这使得传统PIC方法无法直接应用于混合模型。作者想要探究PIC能否惠及混合模型以及需要什么条件。

Method: 提出LinearKV框架，核心思想是"解耦初始化"：每个线性循环层将其匹配的K个局部状态映射为单个初始状态（而非精确组合所有状态），全注意力层则维持原有的KV拼接方式。这使得LinearKV与现有PIC方法完全兼容，可直接复用其token选择和重计算机制。作者在GDN和Mamba-2等多种混合模型上，结合EPIC等PIC选择器进行对比实验，并与精确状态组合方案（HYPIC方法）做对照。

Result: 在两个GDN模型上，单状态初始化和精确组合两种方案表现相当，均恢复约92%的完整质量；但在Mamba-2模型上，精确组合方案在各种选择器下质量崩溃，以EPIC为例仅恢复46.6%的完整质量，而单缓存块初始化恢复86.8%。单状态初始化在计算开销上也更具优势，TTFT降为全prefill的0.46x，而精确组合方案还有额外5%-17%的开销。结果在LongBench QA和RULER的8K-32K长度下均一致。

Conclusion: PIC可以惠及混合LLM，但前提是采用"解耦初始化"而非精确代数组合。单个缓存状态作为线性层的初始状态已足够，看似更精确的完整前缀状态组合方案不仅不必要，而且在某些架构（如Mamba-2）上会显著损害性能。这为混合模型的缓存复用提供了简单而有效的方案。

Abstract: LLM serving is increasingly accelerated by position-independent caching (PIC). Existing PIC methods, however, are built for full-attention models, where a token-indexed KV cache underlies its core operations: matching reusable token chunks, concatenating their KV entries, and selectively recomputing a few tokens to restore cross-chunk context. Hybrid LLMs break these primitives---they replace most attention layers with linear recurrences that expose only a fixed-size state, leaving no token-indexed KV to concatenate or to locally repair. This raises a natural question: can PIC benefit hybrid models, and what would it take? We present LinearKV, a training-free hybrid-PIC framework. Its key insight is a \emph{decoupled initialization}: each linear layer maps its $K$ matched local states to a single initial state, while full-attention layers concatenate their KV as before. LinearKV is therefore compatible with existing PIC methods, reusing their token selection and recomputation as-is. Under this framework, we find that a \emph{single cached state} suffices as the linear layer's initializer. The algebraically principled alternative---composing all $K$ cached states into the exact full-prefix state, as concurrent work HYPIC does---is unnecessary and, on some architectures, even harmful. We compare the two across three hybrid models and three PIC selectors. On the two GDN models the two tie, both recovering most of full quality (up to $92\%$); on the Mamba-2 model, exact composition instead collapses under every selector---under EPIC, for instance, it recovers only $46.6\%$ of full quality, versus $86.8\%$ for a single cached block initializer. A single state initializer is also cheaper, cutting time-to-first-token to $0.46\times$ full prefill versus a further $5$--$17\%$ overhead for exact composition; results hold across LongBench QA and RULER at 8K--32K.

</details>


### [152] [InfraBench: Evaluating Infrastructure Agents Across Layers, Lifecycle, and Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11234)
*Yuan Gao,Zeren Yang,Junnan Li,Shawn,Zhong,Ahmed Dajani,Mai Zheng,Andrea Arpaci-Dusseau,Remzi Arpaci-Dusseau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出InfraBench，一个用于评估AI智能体在真实基础设施管理任务上表现的基准测试套件，覆盖全系统栈和全运维生命周期，并带有细粒度风险评估。实验表明即使最强的智能体也无法在所有任务中获得满分，且智能体普遍存在"满足短期目标但留下非持久变更、破坏分布式不变量、产生不安全副作用和未清理状态"的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算基础设施管理因日益增长的复杂性而变得越来越困难，AI智能体的最新进展为自动化基础设施管理任务提供了契机，但目前尚不清楚这些智能体能否有效应对真实世界的基础设施复杂性，需要一个系统的基准来评估其能力。

Method: 构建InfraBench基准套件，覆盖全系统栈和全运维生命周期，包含细粒度风险评估机制。在15种智能体模型配置上进行实验，每个任务重复3次，使用平均有效分数、标准误差和通过率等指标进行评估，并通过逐检查点（per-check）评分分析失败模式。

Result: 最强智能体无法在所有任务上获得满分。平均有效分数在约40%至88%之间（各配置标准误差为6-12分）；每个任务重复三次后，顶级配置的通过率仍然较低；逐检查点评分揭示了普遍失败模式：智能体可能满足短期目标，但遗留非持久变更、破坏分布式不变量、产生不安全副作用并留下未清理的状态。

Conclusion: 当前AI智能体在基础设施管理任务上距离完全可靠仍有显著差距，尤其是在持久性、一致性和安全性方面存在系统性不足。InfraBench作为公开基准（含实时排行榜、任务与评估工具）将持续推动该领域的研究与改进。

Abstract: Managing modern computing infrastructure has become a steadily harder problem due to the ever-increasing complexity. Recent advances in AI agents create a timely opportunity to automate infrastructure management tasks, but it remains unclear how well such agents can handle real-world infrastructure complexity. We present InfraBench, a benchmark suite for evaluating AI agents on realistic infrastructure tasks across the full system stack and full operational lifecycle with fine-grained risk assessment. Experiments with 15 agent-model configurations show that even the strongest agent cannot secure a full score across all tasks. Mean effective scores range from roughly 40% to 88% (with per-configuration standard errors of 6-12 points), repeating every task three times reveals that top configurations still pass only a fraction of their attempts, and per-check scoring exposes a general failure pattern: agents may routinely satisfy short-term objectives while leaving non-durable changes, broken distributed invariants, unsafe side effects, and uncleaned state behind. INFRABENCH, including its live leaderboard, tasks, and evaluation harness, is publicly available at infraben.ch.

</details>


### [153] [CORA-Diff: Confidence-Oriented Residual Acceptance for Efficient Diffusion Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11235)
*Yifan Wu,Yufeng Zhang,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CORA-Diff，一种无需训练的扩散语言模型加速方法，利用原生轨迹信号（置信度与持续性）识别可能已达终点的token，仅对未决位置应用门控，保留原始传递规则，在保持任务质量的同时显著降低解码延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）逐块并行更新多个token，但实际解码器通常使用固定的去噪视界。许多预测早期即稳定，但逐块解码仍持续到所有位置都被解析，导致重复的密集前向计算。现有加速方法依赖学习过滤器、修改的得分、依赖模型或缓存特定机制。作者质疑是否原生轨迹信号本身就能识别与确定性密集终点匹配的残余位置，从而避免额外模型或修改。

Method: CORA-Diff是一种免训练方法：保留原始传递规则，仅对传递规则未解决的位置应用置信度-持续性门控。被接受的token作为上下文保持可见，块终止条件为所有位置均被解析。该方法不需要修改骨干网络、学习接受模型或修改logits。理论分析解释了高置信度且持续的预测更可能匹配固定视界密集终点，并通过配对的事后干预轨迹提供实证支持。

Result: 在匹配的Learn2PD风格LLaDA协议下，CORA-Diff在8个任务-长度设置中均测得最低运行时间。任务分数在5个设置中匹配或超过密集解码，最大观测下降为1.22分。相对于EOS感知密集解码的增量加速在GSM8K和HumanEval上分别为2.70x和3.32x；在固定视界1024/1024机制隔离协议下达到13.14x；并可在不重新调参的情况下迁移到Dream，获得3.18x-3.53x加速。

Conclusion: 原生置信度与持续性信号能够实现可靠的残余接受，在保持任务质量的同时减少重复去噪计算。CORA-Diff作为一种无需训练、无需修改模型的方法，在多个基准上均展示出显著的加速效果与可迁移性。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) update many tokens in parallel, yet practical decoders often use a fixed denoising horizon. Many predictions stabilize early, but blockwise decoding continues until all positions are resolved, causing repeated dense forward passes. Existing accelerators often rely on learned filters, modified scores, dependency models, or cache-specific mechanisms. We ask whether native trajectory signals can identify residual positions likely to match the deterministic dense endpoint. We propose CORA-Diff, a training-free method that preserves the original transfer rule and applies confidence-and-persistence gating only to positions that rule leaves unresolved. Accepted tokens remain visible as context, and the block terminates once all positions are resolved. This requires no backbone change, learned acceptance model, or logit modification. Our theory explains why high-confidence, persistent predictions are more likely to match the fixed-horizon dense endpoint, and paired post-intervention trajectories provide direct empirical support. We select one operating point on a separate GSM8K calibration subset and freeze it for all evaluations. Under a matched Learn2PD-style LLaDA protocol, CORA-Diff has the lowest measured runtime in all eight task-length settings. Task scores match or exceed dense decoding in five settings, and the largest observed drop is 1.22 points. Its incremental speedups over EOS-aware dense decoding are 2.70x and 3.32x on GSM8K and HumanEval. It also reaches 13.14x under the fixed-horizon 1024/1024 mechanism-isolation protocol and transfers to Dream without retuning at 3.18x-3.53x. These results show that native confidence and persistence enable reliable residual acceptance, reducing repeated denoising computation while preserving task quality.

</details>


### [154] [Geometry-aware Incremental Neural Operator for Long-Horizon PDE prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11237)
*Jiaquan Zhang,Shuxu Chen,Haifan Meng,Yi Lu,Zhihan Lyu,Fan Mo,Wei Dong,Yang Yang,Chaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种几何感知增量神经算子（GeoIncNO），通过增量残差推进、低秩投影器调节频带通道耦合以及均值-波动解耦重建机制，解决了长时程自回归PDE预测中的谱不一致、相位错位和均值漂移等稳定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经算子方法主要改进状态表示和算子主干网络，但忽略了反复应用的潜在状态增量转移缺乏结构化约束，导致谱误差和通道耦合不稳定在滚动预测中累积，进而引发长时程预测的稳定性退化。

Method: GeoIncNO包含三个核心设计：(1) 增量残差推进机制，预测潜在空间增量而非直接预测状态；(2) 轻量低秩投影器，根据增量谱能量分布识别活跃频带并调节通道耦合；(3) 均值-波动解耦重建，对稳定均值结构和动态波动分别融合，仅对零均值波动分量施加相位校正。

Result: 在覆盖1D、2D和3D动力系统的六个PDE基准上，GeoIncNO相比竞争性的神经算子基线，在预测精度、滚动稳定性和频谱保真度上均取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过对增量转移的结构化约束和物理空间重建的均值-波动解耦，GeoIncNO为长时程PDE预测中的误差累积问题提供了一种有效的解决方案，兼具精度、稳定性和频谱保真度优势。

Abstract: Neural operators have shown strong potential for learning solution operators of partial differential equations (PDEs). However, long-horizon autoregressive prediction remains challenging: local errors accumulate as spectral inconsistency, phase misalignment, or mean drift. Existing methods mainly improve state representations and operator backbones, while leaving the repeatedly applied latent transition increment weakly structured, allowing spectral errors and unstable channel couplings to accumulate during rollout. To address these issues, we propose a geometry-aware incremental neural operator (GeoIncNO) for stable long-horizon PDE prediction. GeoIncNO predicts latent increments for residual advancement and uses lightweight low-rank projectors to regulate channel coupling within active frequency bands derived from the increment spectral energy distribution. To reduce physical-space reconstruction errors, GeoIncNO further introduces a mean--fluctuation decoupled reconstruction mechanism, where stable mean structures and dynamic fluctuations are fused separately, and phase correction is applied only to the zero-mean fluctuation component. Extensive experiments on six PDE benchmarks, covering 1D, 2D, and 3D dynamical systems, show that GeoIncNO achieves consistently strong prediction accuracy, improved rollout stability, and better spectral fidelity compared with competitive neural-operator baselines.

</details>


### [155] [Towards Query-Agnostic RAG Evaluation via Query Coverage and Claim Verifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11238)
*Jeonghwan Choi,Taewon Yun,Minjeong Ban,Gyeonghun Sun,Jae-Gil Lee,Hwanjun Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Q-CARE，一个查询无关且完全无需参考的RAG评估框架，通过将查询分解为子查询、将答案分解为原子声明，基于查询覆盖率和声明可验证性实现了细粒度评估，在八个人工标注数据集上优于RAGEval和RAGChecker等现有指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）通过检索证据来提升大语言模型的真实性，但现有评估框架难以在从封闭式事实查询到开放式解释请求的多样化用户查询范围内提供一致且细粒度的诊断。这促使作者设计一个统一的、无需参考的评估框架，能够在不同查询类型上都给出可靠的细粒度评估。

Method: Q-CARE采用查询无关和完全无需参考的设计，核心方法包括：(1) 将查询分解为子查询，将生成答案分解为原子声明；(2) 建立基于查询覆盖率（query coverage）和声明可验证性（claim verifiability）的统一评估原则；(3) 提出覆盖率感知的检索器指标（C-Prec@k、C-nDCG@k）和声明级别的生成器指标（Completeness、Conciseness、Verifiableness）。

Result: 在跨越八个数据集的人工标注基准上，Q-CARE与人类判断的相关性高于四种现有RAG评估指标（包括RAGEval和RAGChecker），证明了其作为可靠自动化评估框架的有效性。

Conclusion: Q-CARE提供了一种查询无关、无需参考的细粒度RAG评估方案，同时涵盖检索器和生成器两个层面，在相关性上优于现有方法，可作为可靠的自动化RAG评估框架，且代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation improves the factuality of large language models by grounding responses in retrieved evidence, yet existing evaluation frameworks struggle to provide consistent, fine-grained diagnostics across the diverse spectrum of user queries, ranging from close-ended fact-seeking to open-ended explanatory requests. We propose Q-CARE, a query-agnostic and fully reference-free framework that enables fine-grained assessment by decomposing queries into sub-queries and answers into atomic claims. Q-CARE establishes a unified evaluation principle based on query coverage and claim verifiability, yielding coverage-aware retriever metrics (C-Prec@k, C-nDCG@k) and claim-level generator metrics (Completeness, Conciseness, and Verifiableness). On a human-annotated benchmark spanning eight datasets, Q-CARE achieves higher correlation with human judgments than four existing RAG evaluation metrics, including RAGEval and RAGChecker, proving its effectiveness as a reliable, automated evaluation framework. Code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/DISL-Lab/Q-CaRE-COLM-26.

</details>


### [156] [VQ-bench: A Composable Vector Quantization Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11240)
*Ashwin Padaki,Amir Ingber,Edo Liberty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的向量量化框架，将7种常见量化原语任意组合，重新表达25种常用量化器，并开源VQ-bench基准测试以促进量化算法的开发和评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量量化虽是一个古老问题，但近年来已成为AI基础设施的核心组件，因此涌现了大量新的工程和研究活动。然而缺乏一个统一的框架来开发和基准测试新的量化算法，导致研究分散、难以比较。

Method: 作者描述了7种常见的量化概念原语，展示了如何将它们任意组合构建量化器；然后用这些原语流水线重新表达了25种常见的量化器；最后发布了VQ-bench作为开源基准测试平台，提供可复现的公开基准。

Result: 成功将25种常见量化器统一表达为由7种基本原语组成的流水线，并发布了VQ-bench开源基准测试框架，支持进一步扩展和可复现的基准结果。

Conclusion: 该统一框架为量化算法的开发、比较和基准测试提供了标准化基础，有望加速AI基础设施中量化技术的迭代与落地。

Abstract: Vector quantization is an old problem but has recently become central to AI infrastructure. It is therefore experiencing a surge of renewed engineering and research activity. This paper provides a unified framework for developing and benchmarking new quantization algorithms. We describe 7 common conceptual quantization primitives and show how to compose them arbitrarily. We then re-express 25 common quantizers as pipelines of these primitives. Finally, we publish VQ-bench as open-source to be extended further and make reproducible benchmarks publicly available.

</details>


### [157] [RecSys Factory: Bounding LLM Agent Autonomy to Decision Points in the Industrial Recommender Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11241)
*Dongyang Ao,Kaixiang Fang,Shijie Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RecSys Factory是一个部署于腾讯三个推荐业务线的LLM代理平台，运行78天。论文提出"自主性-确定性-效率"三难问题框架，通过三种"解构"分别化解三难的一极：运行时解构为三个事件源（零守护进程、等待期零CPU消耗）、能力解构为29文件技能生态（8,971行SKILL.md机械编译为400条PitfallStore）、部署解构覆盖三个标签语义不同的业务线。平台通过人机协同卡片协议保留人在诊断与执行边界。78天内记录1,624次CLI工具派发，成功率78.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM代理部署到工业推荐系统运维中面临三重张力：通用自主性（零样本解释操作者意图、生成胶水代码）、工业确定性（符合schema的特征提取、不崩溃的A/B测试、零合规路径幻觉）和端到端效率。这三者构成三难问题——任意两者最大化必然以牺牲第三方为代价。作者旨在设计一个能同时平衡这三重需求的平台。

Method: 设计原则是"决策点自主，而非管道自主"，通过三种解构实现：1）运行时解构——将系统划分为三个宿主事件源（Claude Code Stop钩子、企业IM webhooks、工作流调度器API），平台在等待阶段无长驻守护进程、在94%等待Spark/GPU作业的墙钟时间内零CPU消耗；2）能力解构——建立29文件技能生态（8,971行SKILL.md），其逐技能陷阱表机械编译为400条目标的PitfallStore，将自主性限制在预提交管道内的有界类型化决策面上；3）部署解构——横跨三个标签语义、A/B层拓扑和操作者画像各异的业务线。此外通过人机协同卡片协议（schema校验、幂等、可重放）将人保留在诊断-执行边界。

Result: 平台在三个腾讯推荐业务线部署78天，记录1,624次CLI工具派发，聚合成功率78.6%。在两个业务线观察到入职时间压缩现象（作为案例研究观察报告，非泛化声明，未与受控基线对比）。8天16轮试点验证了人工在环卡片协议作为审计轨迹原语的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文通过三种解构分别化解自主性-确定性-效率三难中的每一极：运行时解构保证效率、能力解构通过PitfallStore保证确定性、部署解构结合人机协议保留自主性边界。该工作为工业环境中的LLM代理部署提供了一种务实的架构范式，同时作者对研究结论的表述持审慎态度，明确标注了案例观察而非泛化结论。

Abstract: Deploying LLM agents into industrial recommender operations exposes a three-way tension we frame as the autonomy-determinism-efficiency trilemma: general autonomy (interpreting operator intent, generating glue code zero-shot), industrial determinism (schema-conforming feature extraction, non-crashing A/B, zero compliance-path hallucination), and end-to-end efficiency. Any two can be maximized against the third. We present RecSys Factory, an LLM-agent platform deployed for 78 days across three heterogeneous Tencent recommender business lines. The design principle is autonomy at decision points, not over pipelines, made concrete through three deconstructions that each discharge one vertex of the trilemma. Runtime is deconstructed into three host-emitted event sources (Claude Code Stop hooks, corporate-IM webhooks, workflow scheduler APIs): the platform carries no long-running daemon during the wait phase and consumes zero CPU during the 94% of wall-clock spent waiting on Spark or GPU jobs. Capability is deconstructed into a 29-file skill ecosystem (8,971 lines of SKILL.md) whose per-skill pitfall tables mechanically compile into a 400-entry PitfallStore, confining autonomy to bounded typed decision surfaces inside pre-committed pipelines. Deployment spans three business lines with disjoint label semantics, A/B layer topologies, and operator personas; an onboarding-time compression is observed on two of the three and is reported as a case-study observation, not a generalization claim, and not measured against a controlled pre-platform baseline. The human is retained at the diagnostic-versus-execution boundary via a human-in-the-loop card protocol, deployed as an audit-trail primitive (schema-validated, idempotent, replayable) and reported from an 8-day 16-run pilot. Across the 78-day window the platform recorded 1,624 CLI-tool dispatches at a 78.6% aggregate success rate.

</details>


### [158] [The Off-Support Barrier: Why Semantic Safety Constraints Are Not Learning-Problem Invariants, and What Follows for Prior Design, Containment, and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11243)
*Yoshinori Watanabe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个核心结构事实统一解释当代AI安全中的多种现象：语义安全约束（如智能体不逃离沙箱）是“离支持”对象（off-support），即安全谓词B相对于数据分布q与模型p(·|w)的σ-代数不可测，而奇异学习理论（SLT）中的实对数正则阈值（RLCT）是可测的。作者从此非不变性推导出多项推论：奖励黑客与沙箱逃逸的成因、贝叶斯先验与软惩罚在奇异模型中的弱杠杆、硬不变量应置于外部框架（harness）而软倾向置于模型之中、形式化验证可局部证明该类安全约束的原因及其与局部学习系数（LLC）的两点精确类比差异，以及剩余难题（识别哪个离支持区域重要）与执行预测和自指功能动力学的交汇及其SLT分析机制的失效。以2026年7月OpenAI–Hugging Face评估事件为动机案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图用一个统一的形式化框架来解释AI安全中看似分散的多类现象——奖励黑客、沙箱逃逸、先验设计失效、形式化验证的可行性等——而非将这些现象视为独立的经验观察。通过引入“离支持”对象的非可测性这一结构事实，将奇异学习理论（SLT）的工具与AI安全对齐问题联系起来，为安全约束的本质提供严格的数学刻画。

Method: 本文采用形式化数学论证方法：以奇异学习理论（SLT）为核心工具，引入安全谓词B在σ(model, q)下不可测这一关键概念，对比RLCT的可测性与B的不可测性，从而推导出安全约束在优化、先验编码、软惩罚、形式化验证等不同路径下性质的推论。辅以数值实验和Lean形式化证明。

Result: 作者从单一非不变性原理推导出五项推论：(i) 基于结果的优化自然导致奖励黑客与沙箱逃逸；(ii) 贝叶斯先验设计和软惩罚权重在奇异模型中对编码安全约束缺乏有效杠杆；(iii) 硬不变量应置于外部框架（harness）中而软倾向应内置于模型；(iv) 形式化验证恰好可局部可靠地证明，类比于LLC局部固定RLCT，但存在两个精确的类比差异；(v) 剩余核心难题（识别哪个离支持区域关键）与执行预测及自指功能动力学重合，SLT分析机制在此失效。

Conclusion: 安全约束的根本困难在于其“离支持”本质，这使得约束在统计学习理论框架内从根本上不可测。正确的工程架构应是将硬性安全不变量部署在外部框架（harness）中、以软倾向植入模型内，同时借助形式化验证提供局部保证。SLT的分析工具在识别关键离支持区域这一核心剩余问题上存在局限，需要新的理论工具与其结合。

Abstract: We argue that a single structural fact organizes a wide range of phenomena in contemporary AI safety: a semantic safety constraint (e.g., the agent does not escape its sandbox) is an off-support object. Formally, if q is the data distribution and \(p(\cdot\mid w)\) the model, the safety predicate B is not measurable with respect to \(σ(\text{model}, q)\), whereas the real log-canonical threshold (RLCT) of singular learning theory (SLT) is. From this non-invariance we derive, as corollaries rather than independent observations: (i) why reward hacking and sandbox escape arise under outcome-based optimization; (ii) why encoding such constraints through Bayesian prior design or soft penalty weighting has poor leverage in singular models; (iii) why hard invariants belong in the harness and soft dispositions in the model; (iv) why the same B is nonetheless soundly and locally certifiable by formal verification, exactly as the local learning coefficient (LLC) locally pins the same RLCT --- with two precise points of disanalogy; and (v) why the residual difficulty, identifying which off-support region matters, coincides with performative prediction and self-referential functional dynamics, where SLT's analytic machinery breaks down. We use the July 2026 OpenAI--Hugging Face evaluation incident as the motivating case. Numerical experiments code and related proofs in lean are available at https://github.com/xiangze/Preventing_Jailbreak_as_regularization

</details>


### [159] [Towards the Harness of Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11246)
*Qi Wang,Tianyi Wang,Chengyang Li,Shikun Ban,Yurun Chen,Yizhong Ge,Jason Qin,Chengtai Li,Wentao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Thea，一个用于具身智能体的"脚手架"（harness）框架，将机器人能力封装为可调用的工具，并通过场景图作为上下文和评估作为退出码两大机制，弥合软件智能体与物理世界之间的状态读取与结果评估鸿沟，使长时程任务在真实环境中得以完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体的成功证明了"harness范式"——智能体的能力不仅取决于模型本身，还取决于围绕它的基础设施。本文探讨该范式是否可推广到物理世界的具身智能体，并指出物理世界缺失软件世界免费提供的两个能力：读取世界状态与评判动作结果。

Method: Thea框架包含两个核心机制：1) Scene Graph as Context——一种持久的符号化世界表征，向智能体提供世界状态；2) Evaluation as Exit Codes——检测动作何时应终止、评判是否成功，并在失败时诊断原因。智能体循环将机器人能力封装为可调用工具，通过工具组合驱动复杂行为。

Result: 丰富的行为从工具的组合中涌现，闭环机制能够驱动长时程任务在真实环境中完成执行。

Conclusion: Thea证明了编码智能体的harness范式可以扩展到物理世界，通过精心设计的上下文表征（场景图）和闭环评估机制（退出码式评估），弥合了数字世界与物理世界的本质差异，使具身智能体获得可扩展的长时程任务执行能力。

Abstract: The success of coding agents has established the harness as a paradigm: what an agent achieves depends not on the model alone, but on the infrastructure around it. We ask whether the same paradigm extends to embodied agents in the physical world. We present Thea, a harness in which an agentic loop orchestrates robot capabilities, each wrapped as a callable tool. It inherits the core components of coding agents, modified as the physical world requires. The world, however, withholds two abilities that software grants for free: reading the state of the world, and judging the outcome of an action. To bridge these gaps, Thea introduces Scene Graph as Context, a persistent, symbolic representation of the world, and Evaluation as Exit Codes, which detects when an action should terminate, judges whether it succeeded, and on failure diagnoses the cause. Together they close the loop between the agent and the physical world. Rich behaviors then emerge from the composition of tools, and the closed loop carries long-horizon tasks to completion in real environments.

</details>


### [160] [BEST-KAG: Enhancing Question Answering of Building Engineering Standards with Multimodal Knowledge Graph Modeling and Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11244)
*Jia-Rui Lin,Junxi Guo,Keyin Chen,Peng Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BEST-KAG，一个面向建筑工程标准的多模态知识增强生成框架。它通过多模态知识图谱（MKG）统一表示文档层级与异构标准知识，采用规则-大语言模型混合的流水线进行可扩展的知识抽取（构建了包含251项标准、171,652个节点和310,914条边的知识图谱），并基于图谱检索实现条款级可追溯的问答。实验表明其在专家评估及BLEU、ROUGE指标上显著优于主流LLM，最高提升达74.01%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有建筑标准应用工作流依赖基于关键词的文档检索和人工跨条款解读，无法可靠支持多条款推理、多模态知识利用以及可追溯的条款级证据链接，亟需更智能的知识问答框架。

Method: 1) 构建多模态知识图谱（MKG）以统一表示文档层级结构及异构标准知识及其各种连接关系；2) 提出规则-LLM混合的知识构建流水线，实现可扩展的多模态知识抽取；3) 设计基于图检索的知识增强生成（RAG）架构，支持条款锚定和可追溯的问答。

Result: BEST-KAG在专家评估以及BLEU、ROUGE等指标上一致优于多个主流LLM基线，最佳性能提升达74.01%。

Conclusion: BEST-KAG通过多模态知识图谱与检索增强生成相结合，有效解决了建筑标准问答中的多条款推理和证据可追溯性问题，验证了知识图谱驱动的RAG框架在垂直领域标准知识问答中的优越性。

Abstract: Construction standards are critical for building safety and sustainability. Existing standard application workflows rely on keyword-based document retrieval and manual cross-clause interpretation, which cannot reliably support multi-clause reasoning, multimodal knowledge utilization, or traceable clause-level evidence linkage. To address these limitations, this study develops a multimodal knowledge-driven framework that supports question answering on standard knowledge named BEST-KAG (Knowledge-Augmented Generation for Building Engineering STandards). The framework introduces 1) a multimodal knowledge graph (MKG) for unified representation of document hierarchy and heterogeneous standard knowledge with various connections, 2) a rule-LLM hybrid knowledge construction pipeline for scalable multimodal knowledge extraction, creating a large MAG with 251 building engineering standards, 171,652 nodes and 310,914 edges, and 3) a graph-retrieval-based knowledge-augmented generation architecture for clause-grounded and traceable question answering. Experiments demonstrate that BEST-KAG consistently outperforms multiple mainstream LLMs in terms of Expert evaluation, and metrics including BLEU, and ROUGE, with the best improvement up to 74.01% compared to the baselines.

</details>


### [161] [Towards Sustainable Learning in Online Education: A Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11245)
*Chaofan Zhai,Yicheng Song,Ravi Bapna,Junyao Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI-Tutor，一个基于强化学习的在线教育智能辅导模型，通过同时优化短期（知识获取与巩固的平衡）和长期（学习者参与度建模、降低辍学率）学习目标，在23百万条学习记录、33,700名学习者的真实数据集上显著优于现有基线，在参与度、知识保持和最终学习成效方面均表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线教育虽具有空前的可扩展性和可及性，但普遍面临学习参与度低、长期学习效果差的问题，亟需能够同时优化短期与长期学习结果的智能辅导方案。

Method: 基于强化学习的AI-Tutor模型：短期层面借鉴认知理论，在学习新知识与巩固旧知识之间取得平衡；长期层面建模学习者参与度，用于制定维持动机、降低辍学率的策略，从而提供个性化学习指导。

Result: 在包含23百万条学习记录、覆盖33,700名学习者的真实数据集上，AI-Tutor在参与度、知识保持和最终学习成果三个维度上一致性地优于最先进的基线方法。学习路径分析进一步揭示了其对不同学习者的自适应策略。

Conclusion: AI-Tutor通过强化学习框架有效兼顾短期知识获取与长期学习可持续性，能够对不同学习画像提供自适应的、以人为中心的个性化支持，为在线教育的长期有效性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Online education offers unprecedented scalability and accessibility to global learners from diverse backgrounds, but it often suffers from low engagement and poor long term learning effectiveness. To address these challenges, we introduce AI Tutor, a reinforcement learning based model designed to promote sustainable learning by optimizing both short and longterm learning outcomes. In the short term, AI-Tutor draws on cognitive theory to guide learners through a balance of acquiring new knowledge and reinforcing prior learning. In the long term, it models learner engagement to inform strategies that sustain motivation and reduce dropout. These enhancements enable AI-Tutor to provide personalized guidance that fosters both effective learning and sustained participation. Empirical evaluations on 23 million learning records from 33,700 learners show that AI Tutor consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across engagement, knowledge retention, and final learning outcomes. Learning path analyses further reveal how AI-Tutor adapts its strategies to learners with diverse profiles, offering adaptive and human-centered support.

</details>


### [162] [Conformity Mitigations in Large Language Models Lie on a Single Resistance-Receptivity Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11247)
*Zafar Hussain,Kristoffer Nielbo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多模型协作场景中，群体一致错误意见对单一模型正确回答的"颠覆"效应。作者在23个开源模型、19种条件、3个数据集上进行了超过百万次分级响应评估，发现一致错误多数派能分别颠覆22.8%（MMLU）、54.8%（GPQA）和71.0%（SimpleQA）的正确回答，且84-89%被颠覆的答案与同伴答案一致。文章提出"Resistance"（抵抗错误同伴压力）与"Receptivity"（接受正确同伴纠正）双维度评估框架，发现六种方法均落在前者与后者此消彼长的单一前沿线上（R²介于0.80-0.90）。仅"Reasoning"（推理）是例外：在MMLU可自行推导的学科上，它能同时提升Resistance 7.2点和Receptivity 9.6点，是目前唯一能同时改善两方面的干预手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模型协作已成为语言模型应用的重要范式，但现有研究表明同伴意见会与模型自身参数知识竞争，多数错误意见可能颠覆模型本可答对的答案。已有缓解方法仅关注提升模型的"Resistance"（抵抗压力保持正确答案的能力），却忽略了作为协作智能体同样需要"Receptivity"（接受正确同伴纠正的能力）。作者旨在揭示两者之间的权衡关系，寻找能够同时提升两者的干预手段。

Method: 作者构建了大规模评估框架：在23个开源权重模型、19种实验条件、3个数据集（MMLU、GPQA、SimpleQA）上进行超过百万次的分级响应评测。提出了双维度评估指标：Resistance（在同伴一致错误压力下保持正确回答的比率）和Receptivity（在初始回答错误时采纳同伴正确回答的比率）。评估了六种方法，其中四种来自先前工作（如Reflection），两种为作者自创方法，分析其在两个维度上的分布和权衡关系。

Result: 一致错误多数派分别颠覆了22.8%的MMLU正确回答、54.8%的GPQA正确回答和71.0%的SimpleQA正确回答，且84-89%的颠覆答案与同伴答案一致。六种方法的Resistance与Receptivity呈单一权衡前沿，R²介于0.80-0.90。表现最强的已发表方法Reflection在MMLU上提升7.9点Resistance，但牺牲15.3点Receptivity。Reasoning是唯一例外：在模型可自行推导的MMLU学科上，同时提升Resistance 7.2点和Receptivity 9.6点。

Conclusion: 多模型协作中同伴多数意见具有强大的"颠覆效应"，尤其在其不确定性高的领域（如GPQA和SimpleQA）更为显著。现有缓解方法普遍面临Resistance与Receptivity不可兼得的权衡困境，但基于推理的干预（Reasoning）能够打破这一权衡，在模型可自主推导答案的领域实现两方面能力的同步提升。这一发现为设计更有效的多模型协作协议提供了方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in language models have enabled collaborative settings in which multiple models leverage one another's capabilities, iteratively improving, transforming, and extending each other's outputs. Each agent sees what the others assert before it answers, so peer opinion competes with the model's own parametric knowledge, and a wrong majority can overturn an answer the model would otherwise get right. We measure that displacement in 23 open-weight models, 19 conditions, and three datasets, yielding more than a million graded responses. A unanimous wrong majority reverses 22.8% of a model's correct MMLU answers, 54.8% on GPQA and 71.0% on SimpleQA, and 84-89% of the reversed answers match the peers' answers. Existing mitigations aim to increase Resistance, the rate at which a model keeps its correct answer under this pressure, which is only half of what a collaborating agent needs. We pair it with Receptivity, the rate at which a model adopts a correct peer answer after initially answering incorrectly. We score six methods on both axes, four drawn from prior work and two of our own. Each gains Resistance only by losing Receptivity, and their means fall on a single Resistance-Receptivity frontier with $R^2$ between 0.80 and 0.90. Reflection, the strongest published method, gains 7.9 points of MMLU Resistance and gives up 15.3 of Receptivity. Reasoning is the one exception. On GPQA and SimpleQA it trades like the rest, but on the MMLU subjects whose answers a model can derive for itself it raises Resistance by 7.2 points and Receptivity by 9.6 at once, the only intervention we find that improves both.

</details>


### [163] [EvoGraph-Mem: Failure-Aware Editable Graph Memory for Long-Term Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11248)
*Yuxi Qian,Yuxiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于可编辑洞察图的失败感知记忆维护框架，用于解决长期语言代理中记忆质量随时间退化的问题。该框架通过追踪正负证据和激活状态来区分可复用与冲突/无效的洞察，并引入效用感知检索和图形控制器来维护记忆质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强型语言代理主要关注存储和检索过往经验，但存储的记忆质量会随时间和任务上下文变化而退化——先前提炼的洞察可能变得过时、过度泛化甚至有害，反复使用会造成记忆污染。

Method: 提出失败感知的记忆维护框架，核心是基于可编辑洞察图：每个洞察节点追踪正证据、负证据和激活状态；引入效用感知的检索机制；通过图形控制器在任务执行后更新记忆图——保留可靠洞察、归档无效洞察、修订过时洞察、添加新发现的可复用洞察。

Result: 实验表明该方法在不同骨干模型上一致优于代表性基于记忆的代理基线。消融研究进一步证明：仅追加式记忆（append-only）对长时程任务不足，而证据感知检索和图级编辑能提升记忆可靠性和下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 长期语言代理需要显式的记忆维护机制而非简单的记忆累积；基于证据的评估和结构化编辑能有效缓解记忆污染，提升长期任务的可靠性。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for language agents operating across extended interactions and evolving tasks. Existing memory-augmented agents mainly focus on storing and retrieving past experience, but the quality of stored memories may degrade over time. In particular, previously distilled insights can become outdated, over-generalized, or harmful under new task contexts, causing memory pollution when repeatedly reused. To address this issue, we study insight-level memory maintenance for long-term language agents and propose a failure-aware memory maintenance framework based on an editable insight graph. Each insight node tracks positive evidence, negative evidence, and an activation state, enabling the agent to distinguish reusable insights from conflicting or invalid ones. We further introduce a utility-aware retrieval mechanism and a graph controller that updates the memory graph after task execution by keeping reliable insights, archiving invalid ones, revising outdated ones, and adding newly discovered reusable insights. Extensive experiments show that our method consistently outperforms representative memory-based agent baselines across different backbone models. Ablation studies further demonstrate that append-only memory is insufficient for long-horizon tasks, while evidence-aware retrieval and graph-level editing improve memory reliability and downstream task performance.

</details>


### [164] [AgonAlpha: Autonomous Alpha Discovery via Prompt Economy and Scalable Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11250)
*Weicheng Ye,Youran Sun,Xingyu Ren,Shunyao Yu,Chugang Yi,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgonAlpha，一个在冻结研究工件（假设、可执行表达式、平台证据、理由和审查状态）上搜索的alpha挖掘系统，而非仅搜索公式。它是首个结合验证工件搜索、具有重新执行和否决权的新鲜上下文对抗审查器、以及感知待处理任务的并行预算分配的alpha挖掘系统，并提供完整的公开证据链。在WorldQuant BRAIN上的独立部署覆盖5个用户和6个模型后端，达到Fitness 9.50和Sharpe 3.48的SPECTACULAR级alpha。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型虽能提出许多看似合理的交易因子，但自主研究系统还必须能够分配评估预算、验证自身证据、并保存每个候选因子的产生过程。现有系统仅搜索公式，缺乏对完整研究过程工件（工件、证据、审查状态）的系统化管理和验证。

Method: AgonAlpha架构在冻结的研究工件空间中进行搜索，包括五个维度：假设、可执行表达式、平台证据、理由和审查状态。系统包含三个关键组件：（1）验证的工件搜索；（2）新鲜上下文对抗性审查器，具有重新执行和否决权限；（3）感知待处理任务的并行预算分配机制。同时保留每个提交从提示到表达式的完整溯源信息。

Result: 在WorldQuant BRAIN平台上的独立部署涉及5个用户和6个模型后端，生成了SPECTACULAR级别的alpha，其中Fitness达到9.50，Sharpe比率达到3.48，且每次提交均保留完整的提示到表达式来源记录。

Conclusion: AgonAlpha通过将搜索从公式层面扩展到完整研究工件层面，并引入对抗性审查和感知待处理的预算分配，实现了高质量、可验证且具有完整来源追溯的自动化alpha挖掘。该结果表明将研究过程结构化为可验证工件能够显著提升因子挖掘系统的性能和可信度。

Abstract: Language models can propose many plausible trading factors, but an autonomous research system must also allocate its evaluation budget, verify its own evidence, and preserve how each candidate was produced. We present AgonAlpha, an architecture that searches over frozen research artifacts---hypotheses, executable expressions, platform evidence, rationales, and review status---rather than formulas alone. To our knowledge, AgonAlpha is the first alpha-mining system to combine verified artifact search, a fresh-context adversarial reviewer with re-execution and veto authority, and pending-aware parallel budget allocation, together with a complete public evidence trail. Independent deployments on WorldQuant BRAIN produced SPECTACULAR-grade alphas across five users and six model backends, with Fitness reaching 9.50 and Sharpe reaching 3.48, while retaining prompt-to-expression provenance for every submission.

</details>


### [165] [Local verification cannot detect non-transportability: a cohomological theory of context preservation in agentic reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11252)
*Suyash Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI
